Owed at build: whether `AddLocationFinder`'s secret needs its own cleanup when a note dies. CLAUDE lean unchanged and strengthened: this beats embark-time re-picking on cost, honesty and save-locality. AWAITING THE RULING.
#### REGISTRATION CONTRACT — BUILT AND MEASURED 2026-08-18 (three rulings the same sitting)

The v1 ruling (2026-08-12) executed for v2: **carvers stamp each space's identity at birth; the
dress phase never classifies.** Every pocket now carries `role`, `depth` (walk distance from the
mouth over finished floor, not a crow line), `depthRank`, and the pre-existing `live` flag; the
block-yard pocket additionally carries `footprint` (its actual floor cells, so the chalk border
can wrap an L). Site-level `yard` records where the yard landed.

**Three user rulings, all 2026-08-18:**
- **Commons presence: 50-75% of flourishing sites, never 100%** (*"commons i'd lean on having
  accessible more often but not 100%. maybe 50-75%?"*). Measured before building: width-only
  character put a near-mouth hall in ~24% of flourishing sites, i.e. roughly ONE commons per
  world — the leisure tell of the ruled stage axis almost never fired.
- **Block yard lives inside OR outside** (*"the block yard is fine inside or outside"*). Interior
  candidate claims a pocket; with none, the yard takes a patch of canyon floor spreading from the
  doorstep (min 12 cells).
- **The front-third gate on the commons is dead** (*"maybe we're being too restrictive on where a
  commons can be? why does it need to be in the front third?"*). The near-mouth placement was a
  Claude read accepted in DIRECTION (repurposed worked-out face), so it survives as a
  preference — the carve anchors the hall front-first and the assigner takes the shallowest
  hall — but not as a gate. Verified the story holds without the gate: **92 of 94 commons landed
  in the front half anyway.**

**Role assignment (CLAUDE calls, delegated plumbing; precedence order):** commons (flourishing
only, shallowest hall) → block yard (off-route non-hall, front third, nearest) → face (deepest
reachable pocket regardless of character, plus every unclaimed hall) → dwelling (remaining
off-route, door count ignored) → plain (on-route leftovers; dress skips them — RULE 9, plainness
is information). Face-as-deepest means the newest cut is the least developed, which is the
accretion axis told literally; the deepest pocket is a plain room ~half the time and the face
dress table should expect that.

**Mechanism:** flourishing rolls `commonsPct` (default 95); on success a dedicated pass (3000
tries, before the budget loop) seats a hall-width box on the haul road — front 40% of the path
first, whole road after 1500, straddle 2:1 over beside, height gives ground to 5 but width never
drops below `hallW+2` because width is the hall's identity. Tight bands refuse the box ~4 times
in 10 even at minimum size (fail sites' natural pockets max out 5-8 wide against plenty of band
mass), which is the honest-failure channel making "never 100%" true by geometry.

**FINAL MEASURE (700 seeds/stage, true-stage denominators): commons in 55.6% of 169 flourishing
sites — inside the ruled band.** Yard: 52% inside / 32% outside / 17% none (thin canyon floor at
the mouth; presence was not ruled, flagged not solved). Roles per site hold no surprises: every
pocket got exactly one role, 0 doorless, 0 splits, ~4.5ms/zone (was ~3.3; the commons pass and
the depth BFS are the cost).

**The denominator trap bit AGAIN and is recorded as a recurrence:** the first commons census
forced stage 2 and read 12-15%, and two rounds of "fixes" chased it, before checking that
**forced-stage rolls still degrade** — 235 of 332 forced-flourishing rolls came back as smaller
stages and were counted in the denominator. Filter on the FINAL stage (`stageIdx`), not the
asked-for one. Same lesson as the spawn-analogue misread: check the denominator before believing
any rate.

**Also observed, pre-existing, not touched: pocket budgets rarely fill.** Flourishing asks 6-9
pockets and places ~3.6 on average (working ~2.7 vs 3-5 asked); the 900-try loop runs dry in
snaking bands. Roles are downstream of it (fewer dwellings than the budget implies). Flagged for
a placement pass of its own if the dress tables want more rooms.

#### DRESS TABLES PORTED FROM v1 — 2026-08-18 (user docket: "let's port dress table progress from v1 then?")

v1's three dress passes landed on the registration contract, tables and laws verbatim where the
registry shapes match, tagged adaptations where they do not. The roles do the classifying, the
tables never do — the contract working as ruled. **Dress rolls on every build** (census sees it);
the `dress` toolbar checkbox gates RENDER only, so bare geometry stays judgeable (v1 law).

**Ported verbatim:** the socket work-evidence table (one-item-per-space, heavy Nothing, face
hint = light and tools where work stopped, trash middens) · gallery pillar/bay/floor tables ·
the full typed-dwelling grammar (ROOMPOOLS with every 08-17 vocabulary ruling intact, ROOMTYPES
weights, light-first + signature-first rows, budget 0.3 of floor, flat-1 light, threshold guard) ·
the natural scatter (floor roll + 0.03/cell) · the articulation guard for solid items · v1's
SOLIDITY list (statues, stele, largeboulder).

**Adapted, tagged CLAUDE in the code:** face = one socket roll per ~30 floor cells (1-3; v1's
sockets were bites, a v2 face is a room) · commons articulation = one pillar-table roll beside
each recorded pillar + a bay roll at each spur tip + one centrepiece floor roll (v2 pockets now
record `pillarCells`/`spurCells` at carve, extending the birth registry) · quarry-yard recipe on
the FOOTPRINT: chalk only on OPEN edges (the border is the claim; rock needs no chalk), bench
nearest the mouth, chair 70%, basket, stack runs ascending-from-bench per the 2026-07-27 ruling.

**NOT ported, loud on purpose:** the hall/corridor table (v2 registers no corridor spaces) · the
foot/test-dig table · the WARRENFLOOR top-up law · galleryslot (no blind slots in the v2 carve).
**TENSE: everything dresses lived-in.** Abandoned/reclaimed tables are unwritten and are the
next dress sitting — per-pocket `live` is already there to key off.

**Render:** ~~v1's glyph palette~~ **SPRITES INLINED same sitting (user: "import tiles for
them?").** 40 sprites, 7KB raw: 29 extracted game masks (torch, pickaxe, trash x4, figurine,
statues x6, boulders/stones, scrapasan, canvas chair, basket, beds, cots, tables, anvil, oven,
campfire, hookah, cushion) recoloured live through `tinted()`, plus the mod's 9 stack sprites
and v1's chest/stele drawn baked. Tints marked (v1) in the ITEMS map are blueprint truth;
unmarked ones are render choices. Glyphs remain only as the decode-time fallback: **full sprite coverage landed same sitting**
(user flag "some stuff in our blueprint still doesn't have tiles?") — chalk switched to the
mod's 8 directional pieces, brazier found under its real tile name `sw_firepan`
(furniture.json ground truth, tinted the blueprint's &W/R), pitcher / catch basin / village
monument stele inlined from v1's colored art. That last fixed a Claude error: the monument
stele had been wired to the mod's EYE stele sprite, a different object. Audit: zero glyph-only
entries, zero unresolved sprite refs, item variants roll by coord hash like the trash idiom.

**MEASURED, 200 seeds/stage, true-stage denominators: 0 items on the haul road (geology exempt
by design), 0 on the mouth, 0 in water, 0 warrens disconnected by a solid item, refusals ~0.**
Items per site: working 8/18/38 (p10/median/p90), camp 10/19/44, flourishing 15/24/53 — density
climbs the stage axis. Dwelling type spread matches the weights (house ~half). **Density feel is
the user's call and undressed by numbers — judge in Two-up.**

**YARD SIZE BAR — USER FLAG same sitting** (*"the blockyard is looking pretty cramped and not
really like a proper room?"*, with screenshot): the first cut claimed the NEAREST off-route
pocket with no size test and crammed the recipe into corridor-scale spaces. A pocket now
qualifies only at interior >=4x4 with >=24 free floor cells (the v1 spec's own one-row minimum
is 30; dials CLAUDE's), else the yard goes outside. The outside flood also walks THROUGH tree
cells while collecting only clear ground — dogthorn scatter was strangling it at the mouth.
**Measured: 21% inside (min 24 / median 30 cells, all proper rooms) / 73% outside (median 49) /
6% no yard**, against 52/32/17 before the bar. The 6% residue is genuinely cramped mouths;
whether every quarry MUST carry a yard is still an open feel call.

**OVEN CAP — USER RULED 2026-08-18** (*"gen 3 here resulted in 2 ovens - they should be capped
at one"*): at most ONE oven per site (Claude read: site-wide, an oven is built masonry — flagged
in code). Later hearth rolls fall to campfire; the pool filter fires on any row that could reach
the oven. Verified over 390 sites: zero with two or more ovens, 22 with exactly one.

**TWO MORE YARD FLAGS, same sitting, both fixed:**
- *"the chalk lines also aren't really rendering properly"* — they were isolated glyphs, so an
  irregular ring read as scattered white specks. First fix was a canvas line renderer; **USER
  CUT IT same sitting ("cut the current chalk renderer and just use the tiles in the mod
  folder") — the mod already carries 8 directional chalk sprites** (`chalklineN/E/S/W` sides +
  `NE/NW/SE/SW` corners, TerraFirma/Textures, 16x24 masks, tinted white). The piece is chosen at
  PLACEMENT from which way the open edge faces: two adjacent opens take the corner, one open its
  side. The edge test also tightened: an edge is open only where someone could WALK in —
  chalking beside rock, trees and water was webbing the outside yards (28 chalk cells → 6 on the
  same seed, the marks landing exactly on the walk-in gaps).
- *"the quarried blocks also look kind of randomly placed in the yard?"* — the first cut filled
  nearest-cells-first, which scatters. Stacks now place as the recipe rules: **runs of 3 aligned
  cells, one orientation per yard** (the transpose roll), claimed nearest-first from the bench,
  grade ascending outward per the 2026-07-27 fill-ordering ruling.

**YARD REWRITTEN AS THE SKETCH'S RECTANGLE — USER FLAG 2026-08-18** (*"the items inside the
blockyard aren't really respecting the boundaries of the chalk?"*). Root cause: the
walk-in-edges-only chalk ring was a Claude invention layered on a ragged flood footprint, so the
ring was partial and contents sprawled past the line. Killed. **The yard is now a literal
chalked RECTANGLE per the user's TFMAP**: largest clear rect found in the candidate pocket or on
the canyon floor by the mouth (5x5 up to 9x7, trimmed toward the mouth; fallback 5x4/4x5 "ledge
yard" where the canyon floor is a channel, CLAUDE call flagged), full border in the mod's
directional chalk kit, work column and stack columns strictly INTERIOR. Two same-sitting user
amendments folded in: **desk, chair and basket are each OPTIONAL** (rolled ~75/70/75, dials
CLAUDE's; amends the recipe's "workbench fixed / basket fixed") · **scrapasan REMOVED from the
dwelling seating pool** (amends the 08-17 ruling that added it; sprite and palette entry
survive). The outside rect may claim tree cells and the dress pass CLEARS the brush under it —
recipes.md's own word for the yard is exterior *drape*, and a drape stamps over terrain (CLAUDE
read, flagged not ruled). **Measured, 305 sites: 20% inside / 69% outside / 11% none, zero
ring leaks (no yard item outside the rect, no chalk off the ring, no tree survives under a
claim).**

**CARVE REOPENED: BLOCK LAYOUT — USER RULED 2026-08-18** (*"i would be open for reopening carve
to try implementing that"*, after the placement-pass discussion). Layer 2 gains an A/B:
**claim-and-subdivide** (`blockMode`, default ON) vs the box-throwing layout (kept intact at 0).
The new voice is the engine's own — `BuildingTemplate` stamps mass and hollows, `FillRoom`
partitions down to den size (ruins recon 2026-08-12; den threshold 24 is the engine's number) —
and it is ALSO the organblock drawing's voice: rooms implied by division, the door is the gap
the partition leaves (RULES 6+7). Rect-scan was considered and retired: bottom-up best-fit is
nobody's idiom and greedy max-rect flattens layer-2 variance. Mechanics: 1-4 claims flood band
mass along the haul road (mouth outward, so accretion reads), hollow against their own standing
rock, fuse with the artery where the road runs against them, subdivide with end-biased gaps;
slivers return to rock; wet-pocket guard holds; commons = the first wide region on the mouth
side stops dividing. **MEASURED, 250 seeds/stage: pocket budgets HIT BY CONSTRUCTION — working
4.7 (target 3-5) / camp 7.0 (4-7) / flourishing 9.0 (6-9), against 2.7/3.2/3.6 under boxes.
Zero doorless (after the finished-floor entrance recheck in registerRoles - carve-time counts
went stale when repairs bored doors), zero splits, yards 85-88%, commons 75% (band's top edge;
commonsPct dialed 95 → 75 because block halls seat easily), ~4.3ms/zone.** Dress runs unchanged
on top - the registration contract meant reopening layer 2 touched ZERO dress code.
**THE JUDGING VIEW CHANGED — USER 2026-08-18** (*"worry less about the two up challenge and
instead more on the difference between this iteration and the last one"*): the rig gained an
**A/B layout button** — the same seed carved under blocks (left) and boxes (right), same canyon,
same artery, only layer 2 differs. **Measured delta over identical seeds (238 vs 230 sites):**

| | BLOCKS | BOXES |
|---|---|---|
| pockets / site | **6.0** | 3.3 |
| dwellings / site | **2.9** | 1.0 |
| halls / site | 0.42 | 0.32 |
| dress items | 44.7 | 38.3 |
| loops (by stage) | 0.8/1.5/3.1 | **1.4/2.6/5.7** |

Doorless and splits zero in both. **The loop deficit is the one open tradeoff, and it carries a
METRIC CAVEAT flagged loud:** `measure()` contracts each room to one node, so a circuit through
two doors of the same room pair counts ZERO — and multi-door rooms are the block idiom's whole
circulation model (partitions open at both ends, per organblock). Blocks may WALK loopier than
they MEASURE. Double-end gaps raised to 60% of partitions regardless (walkable circuits are real
even uncounted). Whether the measured loop gap needs closing (more crosscuts under blocks) or
the metric needs a multi-door-aware variant is a design read for the A/B sitting.

**RECT-STAMPED CLAIMS — USER FLAG 2026-08-18** (*"the old warrens were more square/rectangular,
and the new ones use a lot of diagonal lines"*): the first block cut FLOODED its claims, and
floods grow diamonds — a drift off the very idiom cited as precedent, since `BuildingTemplate`
STAMPS overlapping rectangles. Claims now stamp 2-4 overlapping rects (each anchored inside the
last so the union stays one mass); the hollow erosion of a rect union stays rectilinear, so
interior architecture is right angles throughout. **Measured by a stair-edge probe (wall cells
whose pocket-floor contact steps diagonally): boxes 0.3% / blocks-as-flood high / blocks-as-
stamps 6.0%** — the residual is the claim skin clipped by the BAND, which snakes because
geology does. Carved right angles against a diagonal seam is arguably the identity statement
(the anti-carve contrast depends on carver rectilinearity); whether the residual reads as rock
face or sloppy carving is the user's A/B call.

**STAGE HONESTY — BUILT 2026-08-18** (user evidence: a rolled flourishing built 107 floor
beside a 93-floor working, the flourishing hall-less — *"this working rolled remarkably close
to a flourishing"*; size, not dress). The worth-the-trip law generalized: **the LABEL sits on
what got built.** New knobs `campFloor` 130 / `flourFloor` 210 (dials from the measured bands);
a site under its label's floor DEMOTES in place — downgrade only (the label may under-promise,
never over-promise), double-step allowed, no reroll (best-of-N stays rejected), before roles
(a demoted flourishing sheds commons eligibility cleanly). **MEASURED: final bands working
70-188 / camp 130-326 / flourishing 224-494 — non-overlapping downward; a flourishing label is
now always bigger than any working can roll.** Consequence flagged: under blind placement,
honest flourishing runs ~1.6/world (rarer than the old mislabeled 2) — WHICH IS THE
SCOUT-AND-QUOTA CASE STRENGTHENED: assignment by band capacity is how the big stage gets
supplied without lying.

**BASELINE CORRECTION, recorded so the ghost stops walking:** chasing a suspected regression
(world census 14 warrens, no-warren 17.5% vs the recorded 5.7-7.8%), the bisect dead-ended
until the morning's OWN pre-edit census surfaced: forced-stage none rates were ALREADY ~16-20%
before any of today's changes. The recorded 5.7/7.8% figures belong to an earlier knob era
(probably pre hug/pinch cost terms). Nothing today regressed site yield; the journal's old
census numbers are STALE for the current defaults. A fresh authoritative census belongs at the
top of the next sitting, before any number from the handoff era is cited again.

**FRESH AUTHORITATIVE CENSUS — RUN 2026-08-24 (dress sitting opener, current defaults, the
owed number).** All 1,251 desert-canyon zones at target 25/world: **14 sited (1.12% of canyon
zones)** — working 11 / camp 3 / **flourishing 0** this run; occupancy in-use 10 / abandoned 1 /
reclaimed 3; payload-bearing 3; mean floor 146 cells. Consistent with the corrected ~14/world
baseline above; the 25 target stays missed under blind placement and a zero-flourishing world is
now an observed outcome, not a corner-math projection — both are the scout-and-quota case as
recorded, which remains NOT GREENLIT (user's call). These are the citable numbers for the
current knob era.

**SIGNS-OF-LIFE / LIGHTING PASS — USER 2026-08-18** (*"working needs more signs of life i think
to differentiate it from abandoned?"* → talked first, then "that sounds fine, yeag"). The answer
turned out to be a PORT, not a design: v1's ruled lighting machinery had never crossed.
- **Torchposts flank the commons entrances from inside** (VillageBase ground truth: the town
  square's doors get light, paths and hut doors stay dark; blueprint = MountedFurniture sconce,
  LightSource radius 6). v1's 08-16 crowding fix carried whole: doorways cluster at reach 2 so a
  wide breach lights once, posts refuse to stand within 2 of each other, a lone flank is fine.
  Dwelling doors stay dark (the bud-door law: dens are quiet). One post at the mouth - the light
  you steer by from the canyon, a ruled fixture exempt from spacing.
- **A DEAD SITE GOES DARK** - the flip that was missing and was actively muddying the tense
  read (abandoned sites kept burning torches): campfire→remains (existing), brazier→cold,
  firedish→cold, torchpost→the base game's own `Unlit Torchpost` (real blueprint, same tile,
  told by tint in the rig), stray torches burn to nothing. Per-space (`p.live`); snapjaw fires
  place AFTER the flip, so a reclaimed site is dark except where THEY light it.
- **firedish ported** (the mod's own light furniture, unused since the legacy camp track):
  a commons centrepiece row, lit only while the site lives.
- **hookah-circle ported** (the user's 07-26 ruled sketch, never implemented): cushions orbit
  the hookah, choose-k of the four orthogonal slots, blocked slots excluded first, never zero.
- **MEASURED, 405 sites: living 1.48 lit posts + 1.05 stray torches / dead 0 lit, 1.48 unlit,
  0 torches; 14 of 14 hookahs carry their cushion orbit; firedishes only in living sites.**
  The strongest life signal remains creatures, which arrive with the population at port time.

**LEISURE + DRINK SITTING — USER 2026-08-18** (*"more leisure spots for worksites? cushions,
benches/klines, or bedrolls? maybe some pitchers of wine and cider for the kitchens and common
areas, and just to have laying around?"*), all built same sitting:
- **BENCH RULING AMENDED — user: "i meant *specifically* the ornate bench."** The 08-17 cut now
  reads: ORNATE bench dead (its own text carries the tomb register); the plain `Bench` (&w/y,
  sw_bench) rejoins the vocabulary. **Kline needed no amendment - it is its own blueprint**
  (`Kline`, sw_cot, &w/W), never in the cut.
- Break spots at the work face: cushion/bedroll/wine rows on the socket table; cushion + cider
  on the plain-room traces ("laying around"). Commons: benches and cushions by the pillars, wine
  at the bays. Seating rooms roll kline/bench in the pool + a 40% liquid row. Kitchens: wine and

- Placed over 305 sites: 80 klines, 63 benches, 77 cushions, 41 bedrolls, 47 wine + 54 cider.

**SCOUT-AND-QUOTA PLACEMENT — USER CONCEPT 2026-08-18, DEEP-DIVED, NOT BUILT** (*"instead of
only trying to place 25, we could place more, and then trim down to 25 warrens that fit our
tables"*). Full design + decomp receipts + sim validation in
`design/worldgen-placement-2026-08-18.md`. **GREENLIT 2026-08-24 (user: "this sounds fine to
implement", after the pass-over-dud sitting and a full walkthrough). SIM MODEL BUILT same day
per the sim-first obligation — the census button now runs BOTH models side by side** (blind =
the shipped mechanism, with its lying-claim count made explicit; scout-and-quota = score
scoutN zones by forced-flourishing build, fill a rolled stage quota biggest-first, site only
capable zones). Canyon determinism verified in the rig: forced stages share a seed's canyon
by construction, so score and rebuild cannot disagree. **First model run, same defaults:
blind 14 sited / 7 lying claims / 0 flourishing vs scout-and-quota 25 of 25 sited / 0 lying
claims / 0 label demotions / flourishing 5 / payloads 13 vs 3 / mean floor 202 vs 146, and
the stage x occupancy grid fills its corners (fl-reclaimed 1 this world). New placement knob
group: `scoutN` 50 / `quotaFl` 5 / `quotaCamp` 9 / `quotaRoll` 1 — ALL CLAUDE DIALS awaiting
strikes.** NEXT: the C# port pass (WarrenWorldBuilder scorer in OnAfterBuild + the derived-seed
canyon so score==build in-engine + TF-VERIFY capacity assert) — deliberate port, sized as its
own sitting; the pass-over popup's fate resolves with it (a claim then only exists where a
warren stands). Original design record: zones build lazily so selection must
score bands AT WORLDGEN; zone-build RNG is derivable (`GetWorldSeed("ZONESEED"+id)+tries`) but
the ambient stream drifts, so **our builder seeds its canyon from its own derived per-zone key,
making the worldgen pre-roll exact by construction**; the scorer reuses the builder's own
degrade logic so score and build cannot disagree; hook = `[JoppaWorldBuilderExtension]`
`OnAfterBuild` (fires after farms/forts claim); `AddZoneBuilder` takes key/value params so the
assigned stage stamps directly; TRAP found: `GetMutableLocationWithTerrain` samples WITH
replacement — scouts must enumerate the pool. **Sim-validated over 5 worlds x 50 scouts:
flourishing-capable candidates ran 7-15 against a quota of 5; every world filled 5/9/11. Corner
math: fl-reclaimed rises ~0.28 → ~0.7/world at quota 5.** Open calls (user's): quota numbers ·
fixed vs rolled quota (Claude lean: rolled) · scout count · secret naming. First impl step when
greenlit: the census button models scout-and-quota (sim leads).

**LAYOUT DEFAULT: BOXES — USER LEAN 2026-08-18** (*"i'm still leaning b generations here,
honestly"*, after A/B time with thresholds and all block fixes in). `blockMode` default flipped
to 0; blocks stays fully alive at 1 with everything it earned (deliberate thresholds, closet
guards, rect stamps, the best loop numbers) - a LEAN can reopen. `commonsPct` default returned
to 95 to serve the box layout (measured 67% commons presence under the flipped defaults, in the
ruled band; block mode wants ~75, said on the knob). Port implication: `ZonePainter.cs` ports
the BOX layout unless this lean moves.

**THE ORGANBLOCK ENTRANCE QUESTION CLOSED BY EVIDENCE — 2026-08-18.** The charter left open
"whether entrances are deliberate thresholds or simply where the artery arrives." The A/B
answered it (USER, seed 597934883: *"the top one feels more jagged... the bottom is more of a
contiguous path with clearly defined rooms"* — blocks 0 loops vs boxes 8 on the same seed):
everywhere-fusion dissolved the haul road into a honeycomb and the global layer stopped
reading. **Blocks now wall themselves against the road and take 1-2 DELIBERATE punched
doorways** (straight-through, one clean cell, spaced apart; a block the road never touches gets
a dug entry from the repair pass). **Result: loops FLIPPED — blocks 1.9/4.1/8.4 by stage vs
boxes 1.4/2.6/5.7; the user's seed reads as road-with-rooms at 8 loops under both modes.**
Honest cost: block pockets settle at 3.8/site (walls eat claim area; some snake seeds run lean).
Thresholds, not arrival points - the ruling's plural ("entrances also") delivered as 1-2.

**POCKET OVERREPORTING CONFIRMED — USER FLAG 2026-08-18** (*"new carving might be overreporting
pockets - i liked the last one more visually in some cases"*): block pockets ran down to 1 CELL
(the sliver guard only fired at claim level, never after splits) — 19% under 10 cells against
boxes' 7%, boxes' minimum 9. So the 6.0-vs-3.3 count advantage was partly closets. Guards
added: a split that would orphan a sub-8-cell part is retried (region stays whole if no line
works), and whole claim components under 8 return to rock. **After: min 8, 10% under 10 cells,
4.3/site — the honest count.** The visual preference lean is RECORDED AS A LEAN, not a ruling:
the mode toggle (`blockMode`) and the A/B view exist precisely so the default stays the user's
call after real side-by-side time.

**ROOM FLOOR — USER DOCKET 2026-08-24 ("making sure warren rooms spawn with *some* kind of
decoration"), BUILT + MEASURED same day.** The warren-floor law one level down. **Measured
first, 497 sites / 1,651 registered rooms: 109 bare (6.6%) — all face (45/649, 7%) or plain
(64/351, 18%), median 17 cells, up to 36; dwellings, commons and yards zero-bare by
construction** (dwelling budget floors at 1; the yard dresses its rect). Cause: a face rolls
its socket table 1-3 times and every roll can come up Nothing; a plain room takes exactly one
trace roll; the SITE floor only fires when the whole site is light and picks misses at random,
so an individual room can stay bare forever. **Built: a room that ends the WHOLE dress with
zero items re-rolls its own table (face → socket, plain → traces, commons → galleryfloor)
with the Nothing row removed — same cells, same hints, same guards.** Runs dead LAST on
purpose, first cut fired 460x from before the natural/trash scatters (rooms that would have
picked up a stone anyway); moved after them it fired **exactly 109 times — the measured bare
set, nothing more. Per-space Nothing weights untouched (the 08-12 freshness law); count
variance survives, only the zero collapses to one. Same-seed carve and all prior dress rolls
bit-identical (the pass consumes RNG only after the last scatter).** Knob `roomFloor`
(new dress group, default 1 = CLAUDE dial). **Verified, 497 sites: bare rooms 109 → 0;
items/site p10/med/p90 26/40/53 (density unmoved); 0 doorless, 0 breaches, 0 disconnects,
0 refusals, 0 console errors; `node --check` green.** A room where no cell is legal would stay
bare and count refused (the 4-cell-closet law) — zero such rooms in the batch. Dress line
shows `N room-floored`. **PORTED TO `WarrenCarver.cs` + DEPLOYED same day (user: "we can ship
it in game and i'll see it there"):** same block mirrored at the same pipeline point (after the

the user's tfwarrens sweep (27 claims, 18 real warrens built): `JoppaWorld.11.9.0.1.10`
(working/abandoned, 2 pockets, floor 73) logged `dress: 7 items, 3 table, 1 topups,
1 room-floored, 0 solid-refused` — 1 firing over 18 sites, consistent with the sim's ~0.1/site
rate at these small pocket counts; every other dress line carried the counter at 0 with zero
MODERRORs.** **Status: `log-confirmed, awaiting in-game verification`** — the user's eyeball
on a floored room is the last box.

**PASS-OVER DUD OBSERVED — USER FLAG IN GAME 2026-08-24 ("this site gave a warren popup when

encounter, secret and map note all attach at EMBARK to all 27 claims; the build runs lazily
on first zone visit and roughly a THIRD of claims refuse ("no face opens onto enough rock" -
both logged sweeps ran 27 claims → 18 real). A refusing claim self-erases its rumor, but the
pass-over prompt fires BEFORE the build, so it can promise hammers a zone cannot pay. **The
comment's word "rare" is wrong by the measured rate (~1 in 3) and is corrected by this entry.**
No honest cheap fix exists: the prompt cannot know the claim is real without pre-validating
the canyon, which is EXACTLY the scout-and-quota design (worldgen-time scoring off a derived
per-zone key, sim-validated 2026-08-18, NOT GREENLIT). Options put to the user: (a) greenlight
scout-and-quota, the real fix, also answers the census shortfall and flourishing supply ·
(b) park the pass-over encounter until then · (c) force-build the zone at prompt time and ask
only if a warren stands (travel hitch, materializes zones early - brute, works) · (d) accept
lying rumors as flavor. AWAITING RULING — deliberately deferred (user, same day: "i'll
generate a few more batches before deciding"); do not nudge, the evidence-gathering is the
user's own.

**YARD GAP + TRAIL + CAIRNS — USER FLOAT 2026-08-24 ("adding a guaranteed gap in the blockyard
with a trail leading to the entrance of the quarry. cairns placed alongside the trail at
irregular intervals"; user's own scope: "previewing on the sim before we implement our scout
path"), SIM PREVIEW BUILT same day, NOTHING PORTED.** Reading stated and built: the gap is
always in the ring; trail + cairns are OUTSIDE yards only (an inside yard's path is the
warren's own halls). Mechanics: gap = the non-corner border cell nearest the mouth, never
chalked, preferring a cell whose OUTWARD step is walkable (first cut ranked by distance alone
and 71 of 128 trail failures were doors onto rock/brush/warren floor); trail = BFS gap →
beside the mouth over open host floor, strict first, retrying THROUGH brush with trample (the
drape precedent) — the mouth cell stays raw per the doorstep law; cairns on the trail's
shoulder every R(3,6) cells, side rolled (spacing CLAUDE dial; cairn art is the boulder
sprite tinted earthen, PLACEHOLDER, sprite debt filed). Render: trail wears the dirt-path
register (`w`/`w` ground dots), paler than the warren's padded interior; knob `yardTrail`
default 1. **Measured, 477 sites / 427 yards / 333 outside: gap present 427/427, trails
282/333 outside (85%; the 15% residual is water/rock honestly cutting the arm), trail len
med 21 / p90 47, 606 cairns (~2.1/trail), tripwires and guards clean, 0 console errors.**
Port debt when taken: DirtPath objects for the trail (the base object IS the register), a
cairn blueprint + sprite, the gap in the C# chalk ring. **NOT YET EYEBALLED — seed 80005 is
rolled in the rig as a specimen (33 trail cells, 3 cairns).** **PORT TAKEN 2026-08-25 — see
the tandem docket entry below (user's cairn sprites landed; deployed hash-verified).**

**ARTERY WINDING (the maxRun switchback cap) — USER FLOAT 2026-08-25 ("more interesting is if
the path wasn't just a straight line ... there are mazes with horizontal passage biases that
would still communicate that message"; stated reasons: more interesting combat encounters
inside + thematic texture; sim rounds greenlit same sitting: "let's try running a few rounds
of this in the sim"), SIM BUILT + MEASURED same day, NOTHING PORTED.** First item taken from
the 08-25 six-item warren docket walkthrough (the mazelike thread; the user picked mechanism
(c), winding, over dead-ends/loops/chew when the forks were laid out). Root cause named before
any knob: the walker already jogged (`wander` 22%) but thin bands VETOED the wind — every
vertical jog left the flood mask and the abandon rule ate it, so flat bands rolled straight
lanes (the user's in-game screenshot). Built in `warren-lab-v2.html`: (1) jogs may now DIG
into owned rock past the mask edge (never the canyon, never its rind, never the border);
(2) knobs `maxRun` (default 8) + `jogMin`/`jogMax` (2-4), all CLAUDE dials: a step that would
extend a carved artery straight past maxRun deflects into a committed perpendicular
switchback. THE CAP MEASURES THE CARVE, NOT THE WALK — recorded so it isn't re-simplified:
first cut counted walk streaks, but shuffled waypoint bands re-cross rows and colinear passes
merge into straights no streak ever saw (cap 8 still rolled a 15); second cut missed turns
landing on an old lane's end and extending it. Horizontal bias falls out free (the goal chase
pulls along the band's long axis), so the haulage read stands; `mouthRun`'s straight doorstep
untouched. New stat row `artery max straight` — the longest straight carved artery run, i.e.
the sniper lane, the judgeable number. **Measured, 80 seeds / 63 sites, cap 8 vs off: sites
over 8 lanes 15 → 3 (residuals pinned against canyon rind, both perpendiculars refused — rock
wins by design), worst lane 15 → 14 (rind-pinned), median 4 UNCHANGED (the carve was already
staircase-y; the knob kills the straight tail, which is exactly where the user's flag lived),
floor 138.5 → 132.5 avg, loops 1.9 → 1.8, zero disconnected sites.** Specimen seed 570168
(camp): 15 → 8, before/after rendered in the sitting with artery view on. **AWAITING the
user's judging rolls. Open and the user's: the maxRun default (8 is a CLAUDE dial) · whether
between-room tunnels take the same cap (they still bore cost-direct; the stronger dose would
live there, asked and unanswered) · whether winding rides the stage axis or stays universal
(the inverted-stage lean from the walkthrough is CLAUDE's, unruled). Port debt when ruled:
the same carve-measured cap in the game-side walker.**
  - **TUNNELS TAKE THE CAP + DEAD ENDS — USER RULED same sitting ("between room tunnels seems
    worth trying out, yeag" + "we could do a few dead ends as well"), SIM BUILT + MEASURED
    same day.** (1) The borers (connectors, crosscuts, repairs; the geology vein exempt) are
    engine-ported Dijkstra with no heading, so the search state grew one: (cell, entry
    direction, straight-run length), knob `tunnelMaxRun` default 6 (CLAUDE dial, tighter than
    the artery's 8 on purpose: nobody hauls carts through a side passage). Straightness past
    the cap costs +600/cell against a wall's 80 — dear, never a ban, so a boxed-in room still
    gets out. THE MERGE LESSON, THIRD APPEARANCE: each path obeying its own cap still fused
    with neighbours into carve-straights no single path drew, so the cost prices the LANE the
    carve will hold (existing colinear corridor floor counted both axes), and only on cells
    the tunnel would newly carve — threading existing floor stays priced by `emptyBase` alone.
    (2) `digDeadEnds`, knobs `deadEndMin`/`deadEndMax` (1-3, CLAUDE dials): dry test-digs off
    corridors, 2-5 cells, ending in rock, stopping one cell short of any other floor (joining
    would be a loop, a different word), never the canyon/rind/border/water; dug AFTER the
    trimmer (a stub is exactly the dead-end leaf the trimmer eats — the sealed-mouth lesson),
    and a stub at a lane's end owes the cap, its allowance shrunk by the floor behind it.
    New stat rows: `corridor max straight` (warren corridor floor only — first cut counted
    the CANYON's open floor and read 63, recorded so the wf() guard isn't dropped) and `dead
    ends`. **Measured, 80 seeds / 63 sites, full stack vs all off: corridor max straight
    median 11 → 7, sites over 10 lanes 37 → 17, worst 38 → 27 (residuals rind-pinned or
    merge-priced through rock the cost model let through — soft cap by design), dead ends
    avg 1.8/site (5 sites rolled zero, allowance-refused, honest), floor 138.5 → 141.1,
    loops 1.9 → 1.8, zero disconnects, zero doorless. Build cost ~60ms/site in the lab (was
    ~17); the port is C# and unmeasured.** Specimen 570168: artery 8, corridor 7, 3 stubs.
    **AWAITING the user's judging rolls on the full stack; the three walkthrough opens stand
    (dial values · stage scaling · now also the dead-end count band).**
  - **WIND A/B — USER ASK same sitting ("can we get some before and afters?"), BUILT.** Third
    pair mode beside Two-up and A/B layout: same seed, current knobs left, the maze stack
    zeroed right, captions carrying artery/corridor straight + dead-end counts. Specimen
    506816 (thin working band) put the soft cap's residual on display: wound corridor 13 vs
    straight 15 — thin bands rind-pin the perpendiculars. Known, shown, unfixed on purpose;

**SCOUT-AND-QUOTA PORTED — USER GREENLIT THE PORT 2026-08-25 ("let's implement it - should
we have a note in the worldgen loading display for it?" - yes, and the note is in), BUILT +
DEPLOYED same sitting; `log-confirmed pending` ON A FRESH EMBARK ONLY (worldgen code; door-A
rule, existing saves keep their old placement).** THE ARCHITECTURE MOVED off the 08-18 doc,
recorded loudly: the doc's determinism twist (mod-seeded canyon) died when the first port
went host-read ("the REAL zone replaces genCanyon"), so pre-rolling cannot reproduce a
canyon the game's drifting RNG builds. The honest replacement, proven viable by the user's
own tfwarrens sweep: **MATERIALIZE the scout zones at worldgen and score the real terrain.**
Flow: OnAfterBuild (after farms/forts claim) · enumerate the mutable DesertCanyon pool
(reflection on the private list; the engine sampler draws WITH replacement, the 08-18 trap)
· shuffle by derived key `GetWorldSeed("TFWARRENSCOUT")`, ambient stream untouched · build
each scout zone + DRY-RUN the carve forced-flourishing (new `apply:false` on
WarrenCarver.Build - full pipeline, zone untouched; degrade honesty makes the result the
zone's CAPACITY, the scorer IS the builder) · fill the rolled quota biggest-first · carve
winners ON THE SPOT (a flourishing slot replays its probe exactly - same seed, same forced
stage) · wire secret/finder/pass-over/friendlySettlements only after the warren stands.
**Overclaim-27 + self-erasing rumors RETIRED as placement** (SettleRumor survives as the
rebuild belt); the pass-over first-visit dud is CLOSED BY CONSTRUCTION - the encounter is
only wired where a warren already exists. WarrenMaker gains a `Stage` builder param
(AddZoneBuilder key/value) so an engine zone REBUILD reproduces the assigned stage. Loading
display: `Scouting canyons... (k/N)` then `Raising quarry warrens...` via
WorldCreationProgress.StepProgress (the lairs builder's own in-phase idiom; no NextStep, the
bar budget stands) - **both lines DRAFT, user voice owed**, as is the standing "a quarry
warren" secret name. Capacity assert: the `[TF-VERIFY] [WARREN] scout:` line carries
pool/scouted/capable/quota/sited/refusals and flags UNDER TARGET. Dials all CLAUDE,
unstruck by the user's explicit word ("strike nothing, judge on a real world"): SCOUT_N 50 ·
QUOTA_FL 5 · QUOTA_CAMP 9 · QUOTA_ROLLED true (the rolled lean). **Costs stated in-sitting:
~50 real zone builds at worldgen (seconds, the loading note covers it; unmeasured until a
real embark) · scouted-but-unclaimed zones stay built as plain canyon · camp/working slots
on higher-capacity zones force their stage, so a rare further-demote can drift the quota
(logged, degrade honesty is the law).** `[verify]` fresh embark: scout line with 25 sited ·
worldgen shows both loading notes · tfwarrens reveals 25 real, 0 erased · a flourishing
site exists and matches its claim · pass-over popups only at real warrens. Compile 0
errors, deployed hash-verified 3/3.
  - **LOAD TIME FLAG — USER 2026-08-25 ("we may have to do fewer than 50, the load times
    are a bit severe. it'd be good if we could A/B test what the times are like when with
    scout and without"), TIMING INSTRUMENTED same sitting.** New `[TF-VERIFY] [WARREN]
    scout timing:` line splits zone builds / probes / claims, per-zone average included.
    The A/B datum comes free: without-scout time ~ the claim phase alone (winners build
    lazily on visit in the old model), so scout premium = zone+probe ms, printed directly.
    If zone builds dominate (expected), SCOUT_N is the lever; if probes do, a cheaper
    capacity scorer is. **SCOUT_N stays 50 - "may have to" is a flag, not a strike; the
    next embark's numbers decide.** Deployed hash-verified.
    - **FIRST EMBARK NUMBERS + ADAPTIVE EXIT — measured then USER GREENLIT same sitting
      ("sure, let's implement that and see how much time it saves"), BUILT + DEPLOYED.**
      Measured: zone builds 20.5s (50 zones, 409ms avg) / probes 1.4s / claims 6.7s -
      zones dominate as expected, premium ~22s. Same line exposed why naive lower SCOUT_N
      is the WRONG lever: capable-fl ran 6 against a rolled quota of 6, zero headroom.
      Built instead: quota rolls BEFORE scouting; the loop exits once covered with margin
      (fl+1 against carve-drift, camp-and-up +2, total +3); stingy worlds still scout all
      50. Also observed in the line, working as designed: 2 camp-assigned zones demoted
      to working in the real carve (built 6/7/12 vs quota 6/9/10) - degrade honesty
      outranks the quota, offered bias-camp-to-bigger-zones if the user wants it tighter.
      **EMBARK HITCH — USER CONCERN same message ("hoping people don't think their game
      is freezing"): fixed with receipts.** Decompiled WorldGenerationScreen: _AddMessage
      DEDUPES consecutive identical rows, _IncrementProgress ticks the bar every call -
      so constant-text StepProgress per scout AND per claim keeps the bar visibly moving
      through the whole pass with exactly one row per phase (the first cut's fifty-row
      spam and the second cut's static freeze were the two failure modes either side of
      this). Timing line now carries "N of SCOUT_N 50 (adaptive exit)". Compile 0 errors,
      deployed hash-verified, `log-confirmed pending` next fresh embark.
      - **ADAPTIVE EXIT LOG-CONFIRMED (embark 2): 31 of 50 scouts, premium 22s → 12.5s,

        assert: quota rolled 6 fl against 5 capable and the unfilled slot EVAPORATED -

        **Timesave menu offered; USER RULED 2026-08-25 ("sure, we can do 1 and 3"):**

**POPTABLE DRESS MIGRATION — HANDOFF WRITTEN 2026-08-25 (USER: "make a handoff aimed at a
fresh agent to do the migration"), migration itself NOT STARTED.** Full brief in
`design/poptable-migration-handoff-2026-08-25.md`. Rides the corrected insight (Claude
claimed engine poptables can't express placement; the USER challenged it and the grep
found 1000+ Hint attributes - AlongWall 231, InsideCorner 59, Center 25, parameterized
Adjacent:90/OutsideDoor:1 - richer than our own hint set). Shape: table DATA (rows,
weights, engine-vocabulary hints) moves to mod PopulationTables.xml; the LAWS (budgets,
one-oven, articulation, re-roll floors, cluster stamps) stay C#; recommended
parse-and-roll-with-our-seed architecture preserves rebuild determinism; sim mirror
obligation and full verification protocol spelled out in the brief.

  **BUILT 2026-08-26 per the handoff (fresh-agent sitting; migration USER-greenlit
  2026-08-25, every dial below CLAUDE unless struck). Compile 0 errors, strict XML load +
  text-rules OK, deployed hash-verified 2/2 (WarrenCarver.cs, PopulationTables.xml),
  `log-confirmed pending`.**
  - **Recon receipts (the handoff's mandatory first obligation), all read not guessed:**
    the engine hint consumer is `XRL.World.ZoneBuilders.ZoneBuilderSandbox.PlaceObjectInArea`
    (area path, fed by `PlacePopulationInRegion` with each `PopulationResult.Hint`):
    comma-separated hints each build a point set, points scoring highest overlap win.
    Vocabulary read from the switch: `AlongWall` = any 8-adjacent wall · `InsideCorner` =
    two orthogonal walls plus their shared diagonal, any-wall fallback · `Center` =
    literally `A.GetCenter()` · `Adjacent:N` = N% chance the item must land cardinal-adjacent
    to an already-placed same-blueprint object (cluster growth) · `LivesOnWalls`/`Aquatic` =
    wall/open-liquid cells. Rect twin `PlaceObjectInRegionRect` carries the building vocab:
    `AlongInsideWall` (rect border), `Interior`/`Inside` (rect interior), `OutsideDoor:n` =
    points flanking the door n cells out on the front side. Read API:
    `XRL.PopulationManager.ResolvePopulation(name)` returns the public parsed
    `PopulationInfo` tree (mod XML merged by the engine loader). Determinism receipt:
    `XRL.PopulationList.Generate` pickone rolls `Stat.NextULong` - the AMBIENT rng - so the
    recommended architecture stands: resolve the parsed tree once, roll rows HERE with the
    carver's zone-seeded R(). Nothing idiom: `*None` weighted sentinel inside pickone
    (base PopulationTables.xml:6852, the PowerGridReplace family; lowercase
    `Blueprint="nothing"` variant at :15021), sentinel interpreted by whoever rolls it -

  ~~**A KITCHEN KEEPS ONE FIRE**~~ — **REPEALED 2026-08-26, USER RULED same sitting

  **USER DOCKET 2026-08-26 (screenshot of an oven-and-campfire
  kitchen: "making it so campfires don't spawn in kitchens that have an oven. is it
  possible with just hints?"). ANSWERED + BUILT same sitting: compile 0 errors, deployed
  hash-verified 1/1, `log-confirmed pending`.** The method answer, on record: NOT
  expressible as a hint - hints (engine's and ours) seat an item on a cell, they never
  gate whether it rolls; the engine's exclusivity idiom is bundled pickone groups, which
  the per-item room recipes deliberately do not consume. An exclusion is an invariant
  over rolls, i.e. a LAW, and it landed as one line-pair in the existing filter idiom
  (one-oven-per-site, chest downgrade): within a room, once an Oven PLACES the pool
  drops Campfire and vice versa, whichever lands first. MUTUAL by CLAUDE reading
  (campfire-then-oven would still build the double-fire kitchen the screenshot shows);
  flags set on successful placement only, so a seat-refused oven bans nothing. Fire
  dish deliberately NOT in the exclusion (not asked; dish+oven kitchens still roll).
  Sim catch-up: dressDwelling in the lab has no such law yet, flagged with the others.
  `[verify]` hearth rooms roll oven-or-campfire, never both; oven+dish still possible.

    now"), all read not guessed:** the row attribute is REAL BUT ORPHANED engine-side -

    `PopulationResult.Builder`, but base PopulationTables.xml has ZERO authored uses
    and the one consumer that receives it (`ZoneBuilderSandbox.PlaceObjectInArea`,
    `string Builder` param) drops it on the floor: vestigial plumbing. The APPLICATION
    machinery is healthy and public though: `GameObjectFactory.ApplyBuilder(obj,
    GamePartBlueprint)` resolves a cached `IObjectBuilder` singleton (trivial contract:
    `Initialize()` + `Apply(Object, Context)`) via `ModManager.ResolveType(namespace,
    name)`; blueprint `<builder Name="RandomTile"/>` nodes use exactly this with
    namespace `XRL.World.ObjectBuilders` (proven live in our QuarryStones blueprints).
    NOTE the one-arg `ApplyBuilder(obj, string)` overload defaults the namespace to
    `XRL.World.Parts` (`GamePartBlueprint(string)` ctor) - a bare-name call resolves in
    the WRONG namespace; use the two-arg GamePartBlueprint. **VERDICT: overseer-as-row
    is green in OUR architecture** - we are already our tables' consumer (the dress
    precedent), so `PlaceTable` fires `entry.Builder` itself through the engine's
    ApplyBuilder; the wrapper is ~10 lines (`Cleo_TerraFirma_Overseer : IObjectBuilder`
    declared in namespace `XRL.World.ObjectBuilders`, the same convention our parts use
    to resolve; Apply = StonecarverEpithet.Build; must stay stateless, builders are
    cached singletons). Bonus: the identical class is instantly legal as a blueprint
    `<builder>` node anywhere. UNBLOCKS clean-migration item (2), per-stage crew
    tables with the overseer as a Chance= row.

**ARCHITECTURE DOCTRINE — USER RULED 2026-08-26, verbatim, closing the migration era:**
> "my opinion on how we should structure the mod is XML > C# > harmony. we should use
> XML and copy base game idioms where possible, where we can't we use C#, and where C#
> fails we genuinely consider what we're doing and if there's any other options before
> moving to harmony"

The escalation ladder governs every future build: engine XML idioms first (verified
against Base, per the ground-truth rule), C# where XML cannot express it, Harmony only
after a genuine stop-and-reconsider. Codified in CLAUDE.md the same sitting. State of
compliance at the ruling: the migration era moved every constant-and-switch data layer
to XML (dress, pools, baskets, board text, crew, placement hints, names, cold forms,
occupancy/stage dials); the C# tier holds handlers, mutations, laws and dev tooling
(surveyed 2026-08-26: no whole file is XML-expressible - the three near-misses and
their grounds are in the sweep record above); the HARMONY TIER IS ONE LIVE PATCH
(`Scanning.HasScanningFor` postfix, "no native hook exists") plus the parked Lithovore
set on quarantine-hold. The gemstone-scan patch's native de-Harmony shape (option b of
the 2026-08-12 sniff-out: Geomancy grants the engine's own `StructureScannerEquipped`
property for the reveal window, cost = scope widening to all visible walls/creatures)
is ALREADY ON FILE awaiting the user's call - under this doctrine it is the standing
audit item for the harmony tier. Distribution-equal with
    the pre-migration pass by construction (same rolls, same order, same percentages
    now read from XML).
  `[verify]` rides the population sweep above unchanged; additionally no `TerraFirma:`
  hint-parse errors in the log.

**BLIND-CLAIM AUGMENT MODEL — outside suggestion relayed by the USER 2026-08-25 ("pick the
zones at random during worldgen, don't check if they have enough space, when the zone is
found by the player, force the zone into a state that works for the procgen"), USER
GREENLIT the sim pass ("let's try this, also"); SIM BUILT + MEASURED same sitting.
Candidate REPLACEMENT for scout-and-quota placement (which stays shipped until ruled).**
Engine license cited first: StockadeMaker (whose priority slot our warrens borrowed) does
Z.ClearBox + FillRoundHollowBox - forts bulldoze the canyon blind; ours is the minimal
inverse. Preceded by the recon that killed full-lazy scoring: worldgen has a hard ~9s
floor if claims must be carve-verified, and the no-self-erase ruling (USER same sitting:
"self erasing means that our generation is lying at certain points, which is not valid")
plus the 6-12s budget forced the fork this model resolves - worldgen drops to ~0s, claims
never lie because the zone is MADE to fit at visit.
  Sim mechanics (`buildAugmented`/`growAugment`, `augTries` knob default 9): the carve
  gets the REAL canyon first with every attempt and honesty gate; only a refusal or a
  build below the claimed stage thickens rock into the canyon, nearest the band first.
  Guards: never the border, never splitting the canyon floor (component check per cell),
  water only on the DESPERATE rung (damming the shoreline; water is not floor so the
  guard is untouched). Four mechanisms found by census and fixed in-sitting, recorded so
  they survive the port: (1) frontier must seed from the biggest band WITH DRY CANYON
  ACCESS, not the biggest band (water-locked giants); (2) target USABLE REACH, not raw
  mass - growRegion rind-excludes rock touching canyon, and fresh augment is all shell
  until it thickens; (3) hard zones need rounds - the connectivity guard rations growth
  to trickles (nemesis seed 465405: rounds of 95/4/25/32/15/9/12); (4) KEEP THE BEST -
  a later bigger augment can shift the carve and refuse where a smaller one stood, so
  the best standing snapshot replays deterministically.
  **MEASURED, 30 seeds per assigned stage, zero failures everywhere:** working 30/30 at
  stage (fires 30%, avg 146 cells) · camp 30/30 built, 26 at stage (fires 80%, avg 218)
  · flourishing 30/30 built, 17 at stage (fires 78%, avg 228, max ~490). Stage
  attainment ceilings are the connectivity guard being honest; over-assignment is the
  mix dial (assign ~9 fl for ~5 expected). Visit-time cost ~70-400ms inside a normal
  zone build. Specimen seed 180158: flourishing, 271 floor, 385 augmented cells -
  **honest view reads clean; 'show augment' overlay confirms most added rock became
  warren interior.** New lab kit: `show augment` checkbox, `augmented rock` stats row;
  forced-stage rolls augment, unforced rolls stay pure canyon truth.
  **OPEN, the user's: (a) THE JUDGING - does an augmented site read as a lie with the
  overlay off (the canvas question this sim exists to answer) · (b) the water-damming
  desperate rung license · (c) over-assignment numbers for the stage mix · (d) the port
  ruling itself - adopt blind-claim, retiring scout-and-quota placement.**
  - **TWO FURTHER CONSIDERATIONS from the same outside contributor (relayed by the user
    2026-08-25), Claude reads attached, then USER RULED BUILD BOTH ("can we tried
    implementing these 2?") - SIM BUILT + VERIFIED same sitting:** (1) **WIPE-AND-REBUILD
    FINAL RUNG** (`rebuildZone`, knob `augRebuild` default on): fires only when the
    augment ladder stands nothing - records the dry border floor as the traversability
    contract, fills the zone with mass, re-opens the contract, carves on the maximal
    band, and cuts 1-wide canyon defiles from each contract cluster toward the mouth
    (Dijkstra, rock cheap and warren floor dear, so defiles wrap the dig rather than
    breach it; port-side the engine's own ForceConnections closes reconnection).
    Verified on the user's own no-site seed 20260818 with the ladder starved: a
    FLOURISHING stood, 425 floor, 6 pockets, contract honored, water remnant intact -
    the zone reads as a pinched-shut canyon with a deep dig, a new texture. Stats row
    reads ZONE REBUILT. Structure-first-as-generator remains DECLINED (rooms-anywhere
    un-earns the band negotiation, the anti-groan engine). (2) **FUZZY TRACING** (knob
    `augFuzz` 35, CLAUDE dial): the growth frontier passes a cell by once with augFuzz%
    chance so the augment seam roughens instead of rounding; second visit always
    converts (the fuzz can never starve the budget); desperate rounds never fuzz.
    Fuzz-lane census: zero failures retained. Both riders ride the same open port
    ruling as the model itself.
  - **MOUTH-ACCESS LAW — USER FLAG same sitting ("seed 898284209 generated without a
    mouth?") diagnosed + USER GREENLIT the cure ("let's try that"), BUILT + MEASURED.**
    Diagnosis: the mouth EXISTED but opened onto a 48-cell basin the augment had sealed
    off from every zone edge - and the unforced roll showed the disease pre-exists
    augment (a river-cut canyon strands a shore naturally; no law ever demanded the
    shore reach the zone edge). THE LAW, in buildOnce for every standing canyon carve:
    flood the mouth's shore, water counted passable (Qud wades); if no zone edge is
    reached, cut a Dijkstra defile to the nearest edge - rock cheap, floor cheaper,
    rock-beside-warren-floor INFINITE so the cure can never mint a second mouth.
    In-engine ForceConnections would brute-tunnel the same case; the defile is that
    cure in the site's own language. **Measured: reported seed cured with a 7-cell
    defile · unforced same seed needed NOTHING (its basin wades out through the river,
    the check is honest about water) · sweep 37 mixed sites: 0 unreachable, 1 defile -
    an insurance law, rare and cheap.** New stats row `access defile`.

**INTERIOR-CHECKING VOCABULARY — outside contribution (code + infographics) relayed by the
USER 2026-08-25, READ + MAPPED, nothing built.** Two Cell extensions parameterized over a
`Predicate<Cell> Basis`: `IsOuterCell` (any of the eight neighbors fails the basis = the
region's own boundary member) and `IsInnerCell` (all cardinals pass; strict demands all
ordinals, non-strict forgives exactly one diagonal - the staircase-wall/corner-worn case,
their diagram's I6). THE THESIS, their F6 diagram: a cell has no answer to "am i inside?"
until a basis says inside OF WHAT - the same cell is inner on the blue-region basis and
edge on the contiguous-wall basis. The basis predicate IS the definition of inside, and
swapping it re-asks the question of the same geometry. Sweep idiom rides the engine's own
`ScopeDisposedList` pool; real usage composes the self-test (their second diagram: orange
= wall cells with an exposed face, buried walls unmarked, interior pocket untouched).
**CLAUDE MAPPING, for the port whenever a shared helper earns its keep: we have hand-rolled
this operator pair at least six times without naming it** - the sim's rind/onSkin check ·
the mouth-candidate skin filter · draw()'s mass-near-floor crop · the pocket wall ring ·
the augment frontier · the moss halo's "accessible face" clause (plus the trimmer chasing
inner-cells of floor). Converging them on one named pair would make each pass's basis
predicate self-documenting. **Door quietly opened: the chalk plot as a basis** - the 08-18
"items not respecting the chalk" fix was rectangle membership; `IsInnerCell` over a plot
cell-set asks it properly for ANY shape, so non-rectangular yards become possible someday
with no new containment logic. Same contributor as the blind-claim model and its riders.
- **Also from the same exchange:** (1) `GetCellsInACosmeticCircle(Silent)` - an ellipse in
  cell space (y squashed) so a circle reads ROUND ON SCREEN under Qud's tall tiles; the
  Silent variant exists because the assembly's own area getters chatter into the log,
  which for us would pollute the TF-VERIFY watcher too. Their 4/3 aspect vs our lab's
  1.5 is an eyeball knob when a consumer exists; cosmetic shapes only, mechanical radii
  stay grid-true. (2) **PREFAB RPM SETPIECES, padded and cased** - small hand-authored
  map chunks padded 1-2 cells, encased in shale that fuses into the carve's rock
  (needs the fissure wall's PaintWith/PaintPart fusion treatment at the seam), doored by
  the carver's own connection machinery. CLAUDE READ: adopt as the PAYLOAD channel when
  that lane lands, never the skeleton (band negotiation stays the identity engine) -
  and it is the engine-native TFMAP workflow: THE USER DRAWS THE CHAMBER (mapgen is the
  user's authorship lane), the carve seats it as a pocket. The figurine-table chamber,
  overseer quarters and shrine rooms are the obvious first tenants. UNRULED.
- **VOCABULARY INTEGRATED — USER RULED same sitting ("can we integrate this into our own
  code to clean it up a bit? fine if it causes some behavior to change"), BUILT + VERIFIED
  both sides.** Sim gains `touches4/8`, `isOuterOf`, `isInnerOf`; C# WarrenCarver mirrors
  `Touches4/8` + `IsInnerOf`. Converted: the rind/onSkin checks (x3 sim, x1 C#) · the
  tunnel-cost skin toll (both) · the mouth-candidate skin filters (both, incl. growAugment's
  dry-band pick) · both draw() crops (shoreline mass renders, enveloped core voids) · the
  aquifer interior filter, now the isInnerOf showcase on both sides ("sealed pools seed only
  in the enveloped core of the mass"; its border nuance is neutralized by the existing x>4
  margins). NOT converted, deliberately: box-range nearness tests, accumulation loops, the
  count-graded dress hints (corner/wall need counts, not booleans), and SolidOK
  (articulation is not morphology). Verified: C# compiles 0 errors, both pinned specimens
  reproduce BIT-EXACT post-refactor (180158 flourishing/385 aug/271 floor · 898284209
  working/no defile); the fl-lane census reads 18/12 vs the pre-fuzz 17/13 baseline, which
  is the fuzzy frontier's own RNG jitter, licensed. Deployed hash-verified.

**YARD PLACEMENT: AREA DECIDES — USER FLOAT 2026-08-25 ("what if we improved blockyard
placement a bit when there's lots of open space outside?", with an in-game screenshot of a
yard wedged in a cramped chamber beside a wide-open doorstep), SIM BUILT + MEASURED same
sitting, PORT AWAITING the user's nod.** Root cause: the placer took any qualifying pocket
UNCONDITIONALLY (first 5x5 clearing wins) and only consulted the doorstep when no pocket
qualified - the doorstep was never measured. Now both candidates are measured every time
and the doorstep wins when STRICTLY bigger; inside keeps ties (a sheltered yard matching
the doorstep's size earned its spot). No new knobs - rects already cap at 9x7, so "lots of
open space" is just "the doorstep hit the cap and the pocket didn't". **Measured, 200
seeds / 147 yards: inside share 22% (the 08-24 census) → 8.2% (12 yards), outside 78% →
91.8%; the ~14% that flipped are exactly the cramped-inside-roomy-outside class. The 12
inside survivors all beat their doorstep on area.** Port debt when nodded: the same
compare in WarrenCarver's roles pass. **PORTED same sitting (user: "probably fine to
port"); compile 0 errors, deployed hash-verified, `log-confirmed pending`.**

#### PARKED — ~~NEXT TRACK~~ **QUD TILES + CANYON LANDED 2026-08-18 (see the canyon section above). RIVER STILL PARKED.** (user, 2026-08-18: "for another turn, probably, i want to re integrate the qud tiles, canyon and river generation from the base game")

Not started, deliberately. All three exist in v1's rig and all three are **bucket-1 engine ground truth**, so they carry across the branch without re-earning — this is a port into the v2 lab, not a redesign:
- **Qud tiles** — walls drawn with the real extracted sprites, masked off neighbours exactly as `ZoneManager.PaintWall` does (N NE E SE S SW W NW, off-map reads 0); floors rolling one of four `sw_ground_dots` variants per the `DirtPath`/`DirtFloor` `RandomTileOnMove` idiom. Sprites come from `design/extract-qud-walls.py`; `design/tiles/walls/` is gitignored, so the lab must degrade with a pointer when the folder is empty (v1 already does).
- **Canyon** — the decompiled `CanyonBuilder` (flat-98 `AddNoise` blob field, stairs count as connections, 4x4 hub only on the Start branch, `GetLocalAdjacentCells` as a clamped Chebyshev box).
- **River / marsh** — the 2026-08-12 re-decompiled `RiverBuilder` + `Genkit.CellularGrid`.

**Design consequence to settle when it lands:** the v2 generator is host-agnostic by construction (the artery gets a FOOTPRINT and works inside it), so a canyon host means the footprint is chosen from band mass rather than from open zone. That is the one real integration decision; everything else is a render/port.

#### DRAWING PROTOCOL — amendments earned in practice 2026-08-17/18

- **Transfer by file, not by chat attachment.** A 1:1 PNG pasted into chat arrives at ~20px and is unreadable; Claude cannot zoom an attachment. Files on disk get decoded cell-exact with PIL (12.1.1, python 3.12.6, already installed) — every readback in this session was counted, not eyeballed.
- **The readback loop ran for real and earned its place.** Low-res reads of both primitives were structurally right and wrong on specifics; the 1:1 pass corrected the artery from "rooms hung off a corridor" to a single serpentine thread with one dead-end leaf, and reclassified the pillars from aesthetic to structural.
- **Multiple drawings per image HELP when each carries its own word.** Contrast is where the signal is (`organblock` vs `artery` taught the distinction neither taught alone). The failure mode is unlabelled multiples, not count. Ceiling ≈ 3-4 per image.

### Rulings
- [x] `[design]` **NPCs use their mutations — RULED 2026-07-22.** Any mutation a stonecarver type carries, its AI must actually cast — "gotta be able to fight back" against a mutant who opens fire on them. Scope consequence: whichever mutations land on the roster commit their AI work (Fissure: done; EB: advertisement handler + NPC toggle handling; Stoneshaper: NPC branch for the player-only cell-picking path + wall-and-muse targeting, the most complex). These handlers are shared infrastructure — the parked "wild creatures with innate mutations" design-budget item inherits them for free.
- [x] `[design]` **Reception — RULED 2026-07-22: neutral at baseline, trending positive.** At the start you're just another wandering adventurer — they don't start combat; at minimum they can trade with you. Implies a proper faction entry (neutral standing needs a home faction; killing quarriers for their lumps costs rep — the temptation loop is intended). **AMENDED 2026-08-11 (vine sitting): the trade half relocates — wandering packs are neutral-NO-trade; trade lives in camps on the vine merchant (see Creature roster). Neutrality, faction entry, and the temptation loop all stand unchanged.** **SUPERSEDED 2026-08-29 (faction docket): packs open WARY, InitialPlayerReputation -200, the Bey Lah precedent - see the Quarriers section + Factions.xml.**
- [x] `[design]` **Water ritual — RULED 2026-07-22: yes.** Engine hook verified: `GivesRep` part on the legendary blueprint (Snapjaw Hero 0 precedent) puts the faction on the reputation screen, makes kills cost rep, and makes the legendary water-ritual-capable.
- [x] `[design]` **Pack structure — RULED 2026-07-22: legendary-pack shape.** Several distinct stonecarver types divvying up the work (variety is the point), one legendary leading them — exactly the base game's wild-legendary idiom. Verified: `SnapjawParty1` = pickeach roles at varied chances + `Snapjaw Hero 0` at Chance 3; a `-with-Warlord` guaranteed-leader variant table exists as precedent; hero blueprint = base-role inherit + hero `builder` (name/stat boosts) + `GivesRep` + `Role=Hero` tag.
- [x] `[design]` **Legendary naming — RULED 2026-08-14 (naming session, user: "i feel like this works and we can bank it"): bespoke `Naming.xml` style, NOT the default.** Do-nothing fallback verified = `Qudish` style (scope `Genotype="Mutated Human"` Priority 50) — legendaries would read as *locals*, against the across-the-Moghra'yi identity. Shape ruled: **etruscan-chopped personal name + english material-craft byname** (source box: mysteriousetruscans.com/names.html, user-supplied; etruscan = isolate language known from tomb inscriptions — the resonance is the point). Engine ground truth (decomp `NameStyle.cs:221`, `Grammar.cs:114`, 2026-08-14): **the generator has NO phonotactic filter** — sole guard is an 8-entry slur blocklist with a 1000-reroll loop (fails loud: name becomes `NameGenBadWordsFail{n}`); all base-game quality control is bank curation, so scrubbed banks are the defense, by construction.
  **Personal banks (ACCEPTED as-drafted 2026-08-14; junction architecture: C-final prefixes + VCV infixes ×0–1 + C-initial postfixes = every seam C/V, pileups impossible):**
  - prefixes ×12: `ar vel lar than ram sem cul tles pev ras mun spur`
  - infixes ×13: `utha athi eni ena ari ithi aru eli` + **five adds 2026-08-15 (user-blessed after demo): `anthi elthu arna uni avi`** — VCCV pieces restoring the th-cluster growl mid-name where junctions stay safe (elthu = the Velthur treasure rehomed away from its Arthur problem; uni = the attested chief goddess; avi = attested "years," the tomb-inscription word). Repetition dial noted: doubles live in the no-infix space — shipping `InfixAmount="1"` or `"0-2"` is the lever, a length-taste call at build.
  - postfixes ×9: `tha thia na nia ce za til sa cna`
  - Crime sweep: full 108 two-piece enumeration eyeballed clean. Removed in drafting: `ar+thur`=Arthur (thur cut), `vin+ce`=Vince (vin→mun), `se`/`her` (Secure/Here/Hera), `au` (Auth), bare `n` (triple-n). **Flagged, not vetoed, at bless — standing unless user reopens:** Arsa (`ar+sa`, rude-adjacent), Ramza (`ram+za`, FF Tactics), `ithi+thia` seam stutter (rare), overall register softer than early drafts (growly `nth`/`th` postfixes died in the scrub; th lives on in `tha`/`thia`).
  - Feature, not bug: banks can resurrect attested names whole — Lartha, Larce, Rasce, Pevtha, Tlesna (real family name). User-enjoyed.
  **Byname banks (user-curated to 12×12; sized for base-game parity — base hero title/epithet banks run 4–13 values, snapjaw = 7×6):**
  - materials: `stone gravel cobble spall scree flint grit cairn quern karst lode salt`
  - crafts: `smith augur wright knapper reeve ward delver graver scribe reader carter setter`
  - Curation rulings (user, 2026-08-14): **steward→ward** (Woodward/Hayward surname formation; distinguished from the 07-22 warder rejection — embedded byname, not a standalone role label next to base wardens) · **gaffer cut** · **tooth→setter** (bank went all-occupational; the body-epithet register exited the random banks entirely) · **hands reserved out of the random bank** pending the easter-egg call. Register notes on record: augur = the actual etruscan national craft (disciplina etrusca); salt = the Moghra'yi crossing; quern ↔ the mod's stone mill.
  - **TWO-AXIS AMENDMENT — RULED 2026-08-15 (same sitting as name-drives-kit; user direction: "steal the combinative part of goatfolk heroes where the clan has an effect, for our prefixes"). The byname's MATERIAL half now grants a perk (the clan-effect axis) while the CRAFT half grants the kit — one compound word is the whole character sheet.** Builder tools all precedented in `GoatfolkHero1`: `BoostStat` (AV is a stat — rides it fine), resist stats, `Stat.Random(1,2)` rolled amounts (Clovenhorn's horn level), multi-grant, gear populations. Bank sizes amended from the 08-14 12×12:
    - **materials — FINAL 13 (user directive 2026-08-15: parity with the craft bank, wide stat variety; an interim 8-word tightening lived and died the same sitting — synonym-debt dissolved once every word carried a distinct perk):** `stone flint scree cairn grit chalk spall marl cobble salt karst quartz lode`. Permanent cuts: gravel, whet + **quern→grit swap (user-ruled)** — grit's word *is* its perk (grit = english for willpower; the pun teaches the stat); cut vocab lives on in etch-text/description prose. RIP Quernaugur. **ashlar drafted for +DV, user-vetoed ("doesn't slot cleanly") → spall (Claude slate, user-accepted)**.
    - **material→perk table (magnitude discipline: singles ~+2, combos +1/+1 — goatfolk precedent, Stargazer +2 single vs Whitetongue triple +1s):** stone **+1 Str/+1 Tou** (combo; the bruiser pair) · flint +2 Agi · **scree +1–2 AV rolled, PURE — +HP rider CUT (user-ruled)** · **cairn +2 Ego (user cut the MoveSpeed draft; cairns are memory, ego is the water-ritual stat)** · grit +2 Will · chalk +2 Int (the writing stone) · **spall +2 DV** — scree/spall = the two defenses split by how rock fails: scree soaks the hit, spall sheds it · marl +HP (the fertile stone) · cobble +MoveSpeed (the paved-road word — MS landed where it always belonged) · **salt heat 50 (user-ruled)** · **karst cold 50 (user-ruled; Night Vision rider CUT by user)** · quartz elec 50 (piezoelectric — the resist trio = the faction's weather report: sun above, chill below, storm) · lode wealth (richer gear populations — feeds the temptation loop). Non-user-numbered rows are Claude drafts standing unvetoed; numbers finalize at builder implementation.
    - **crafts 12→13:** EB column evened to 3 (user direction: even out, geomancy+office stay low) — **+slinger (Claude word-draft, unvetoed** — the honest ancient stone-missile trade; Gritslinger/Flintslinger). Columns: Fissure 3 (smith/knapper/delver) : Stoneshaper 3 (wright/setter/graver) : EB 3 (ward/carter/slinger) : Geomancy 2 (augur/reader) : office 2 — **split (Claude draft, unvetoed): reeve +Ego/+Will (the commander), scribe +Int/+Will (the lettered one)**. **Word count per column = spawn weight** — the bank is the loot table; weight edits are vocabulary edits.
  - [x] `[design]` **Byname delivery — RULED 2026-08-15: hero epithet, via the goatfolk-builder structure below** (the epithet-vs-`TwoNameChance` fork closed by construction — `GoatfolkHero1` names via `MakeEpithet`).
  - [x] `[design]` **NAME-DRIVES-KIT STRUCTURE — RULED 2026-08-15 (user: "sounds fine to bank"; supersedes the 07-22 overseer mutation-pool ruling's pool boundary).** Ground truth first (decomp `GoatfolkHero1.cs:28-92`): the base game's goatfolk builder rolls the EPITHET FIRST, then a switch on the name text GRANTS what it promises (Stargazer→LightManipulation+INT, Twicetalker→TwoHeaded+horns item, Souldrinker→LifeDrain, Clovenhorn→Horns+Charge skill; clan titles grant too — Clan Sol→PhotosyntheticSkin, Clan Whitetongue→mental stats). Mamon Souldrinker drains life BECAUSE the generator named him Souldrinker. Ours mirrors it: bespoke overseer builder (absorbs the ruled ~20-line picker) rolls the material×craft epithet, then `Contains` on the craft half grants the kit. **The epithet bank IS the pool manifest** — a mutation is rollable iff a byname word vouches for it; user's "every pack mutation + a few base-game guests possible" is delivered by coining words, and the NPCs-use-their-mutations bar becomes the sole gate (a word may not enter the bank until its mutation's NPC AI is committed). Ordering race: none — naming precedes grants inside the builder. Gorgon landing strip: now routes through the bank (give the gaze a word → the two stranded 07-24 verifies get their NPC).
    - **Word↔grant mapping — RULED 2026-08-15 (user: "the word mapping is fine"): the drafted mapping IS the bank; wording stays the user's to polish at the text pass. ONE SLOT DEFERRED by the same ruling — Gorgon's Gaze has no word yet (user: "we can decide a word for gorgon's gaze at some point"), and until it gets one the two stranded 07-24 gorgon verifies still have no NPC to land on.** Mapping as drafted: knapper/delver/smith → Fissure · wright/setter/graver → Stoneshaper · ward/carter/slinger → EB · augur/reader → Geomancy (user's float 2026-08-15 — geomancy = literal earth-divination, `EarthyVibrations` scan) · reeve/scribe → no mutation, office stat-boosts instead (+Ego/+Will class, Clan Whitetongue precedent; user queried reeve→geomancy, Claude counter: office words stay unscoped — kit words vs office words is the grammar, user did not push back). ### Open decisions (one at a time)
- [ ] `[design]` **ON THE TABLE (user's question): how do they get the quarried stone *out* of Qud?** Canon recon 2026-07-22 (Books.xml, *Across Moghra'yi* Vols. I–III): the only established overland route in/out of Qud is crossing the Moghra'yi, the Great Salt Desert; the canonical crossing traffic is **dromad merchant caravans** ("accounts given by fungi who crossed Moghra'yi on the backs of dromad merchants"; "O, how the dromads know!"). Named far lands beyond the desert: **the Sunderlies** (Vol. I — "towering sundered bluffs… canyon walls dotted with innumerable cave-mouths… colossal, earthly honeycombs," cave-clan freeholds, Clan Sotur descends from "Sotur Stonehands," sigil = a sundered mountain), **Athenreach** (Vol. II), **Oth, the Free City** (Vol. III). Mechanically nothing ever leaves the map — the answer only needs to manifest in what the player sees (direction of travel, pack-animal entries in the party table, camp contents, conversation text).
  - **User's current thinking (2026-07-22, slept on it, NOT yet ruled):** (1) stonecutters travel with dromad merchants sometimes; (2) they have their own beast of burden for transporting stone — maybe a vehicle with an interior; (3) maybe the beast is a huge mole-like thing that uses its claws to help dig out quarriable stone when it's not carrying it.
  - [x] `[design]` **Mole-beast RULED 2026-07-22: committed.** The stonecarvers' beast of burden is the huge mole-like digger. **No interior** (cut — cargo, not walk-in). **Burrowing via BurrowingClaws: in, but provisional** — may be cut later if it misbehaves (PathAsBurrower is the dial). OPEN: what makes the mole *Qud* — user wants it weirder than a merely-big animal; weirdness axis unruled.
  - **Mole-beast feasibility (2026-07-22, XML ground truth):** no mole-anything in basegame. The only civilized burrowers are the four mopango blueprints (a friendly *pilgrim* people — canon resonance noted) — all carry `BurrowingClaws`, and the AI dial is on the mutation tag itself: `PathAsBurrower="true"` = genuinely tunnels (pathfinder treats walls as diggable), `"false"` = claws without map-eating pathing (mopango use both deliberately). "Huge" = tile art + description + stats; all creatures are one cell, no multi-tile tech. "Digs out quarriable stone" has two altitudes: flavor-only (pure XML) vs mechanically real (small C# hooking its wall destruction to yield rock items — mod already owns wall↔rock machinery). `Interior` composes orthogonally with claws/brain.
  - **Feasibility recon (2026-07-22, XML ground truth):** *Dromad ride-alongs* — wilderness tables seed `DromadTrader` groups at Chance 2; trader blueprint carries a `DromadCaravan` part that conjures the entourage. Shape (a): dromad-trader entry in our own pack table — pure XML, nothing to verify. Shape (b): merge stonecarvers into the caravan entourage — depends on whether `DromadCaravan` is table-driven or hardcoded (one-function decompile check at implementation). *Interior beast* — `Interior` part is XML-attachable to any creature/object (`Cell` → merged `<cell>` in Worlds.xml `Interior` world → `.rpm` map, same tech as camp chunks; mecha cockpit 5x3, Mover atrium 16x14). `Vehicle` part (pilots, power, EMP) is separate and optional — a living beast needs only `Interior`. Caveats: no basegame precedent for an *autonomous wandering* creature with an interior (mechas move piloted, other interiors are stationary) — decompile + smoke-test "interior follows wanderer" and "beast dies: what happens to interior contents" before spec. Design note: stone stacked inside the beast = the temptation loop, one layer deeper.
  - **Beast REVISED 2026-08-07 (carve-jam session, user direction): the mole becomes a giant STONE-EATING CENTIPEDE.** Supersedes the mole body plan in the 2026-07-22 ruling (the beast-of-burden commitment itself stands; BurrowingClaws-for-pathing dial carries over). User's fiction (user owns concept): vast — "potentially spanning a mile" — a walking wagon for the caravan, outfitted with months-if-not-years of supplies from home; quarried stone spread evenly along its back so as not to crush it, doubling as protective-but-breakable armor; mouth = darner dragonfly nymph labium × excavator; legs instead of hands. Stone-eating ties it to the Lithovore biology — dig-is-eat, one organ, one gesture (Claude framing, user-aligned). Carve-signature dials (DRAFT, Claude numbers-territory): wide low bore, sinuous low-frequency weave, walls smoothed with fine parallel leg-striations, ZERO spoil + polished-pebble gastrolith dung as trail (the dung rhymes with the feeding-sty forage note in the room jam).
    - [x] `[design]` **Single tile RULED 2026-08-07 (user: "that's what qud would do")** — star-kraken idiom: vast in fiction, one tile in play, the mile lives in prose. **Claude's head-tile + scenery-segment-chain proposal CUT by this ruling** — no multi-object body, ever. Consequences: cargo-armor = breakable equipped armor and/or inventory death-drops on the one creature (no statue-spill machinery needed); the caravan-robbing temptation stays on pack lumps + the beast's own inventory/armor.

User's words: "could also see the case for frontal claw natural weapons."
