Verified 16 combs: 16/16 attach, 11 wraps, 4 loops, 20 stubs, 3 rakes (rake rate is the loose dial — needs a straight run + unvisited space; tune against next drawings if the user wants it commoner).
    - **CALIBRATION ROUND 4, 2026-08-08 (user's fifth drawing, self-flagged "a bit too symmetrical but was fun to make"):** negative example banked — GLOBAL symmetry is off-target, but the drawing's gate-like read yielded the salvage: **symmetry belongs at thresholds.** New move, **flanked threshold** (25% roll, ~11% of camps after fit guards): mirrored 1-cell niches at the home pocket's ENTRY-CELL corners (±2 lateral) — the carved sockets the boundary-eye steles will stand in at dressing time (ruled camp dressing already flanks mouths with steles; the carve now sometimes pre-shapes it). Two placement bugs found en route via cell-probe: corridor-end threshold cells sit in ragged-edge territory (laterals often already open) AND inside mouth-weathering's blast ring — niches moved fully inside to blob-validated wall. Drawing 5's dotted RUNS (stacked posts in series) noted as already-covered: the 3x2 room dilation yields a 2-post colonnade row; no new code. Verified 35 camps: 4 flanked, 0 sealed.
    - **CALIBRATION ROUND 5, 2026-08-08 (user's sixth drawing, "another rough sketch"): the RANKS** — the drawing's right side is the same unit family SERIALIZED: repeated ring/C-bracket rooms at regular spacing off a straight lane — the honeycomb as housing block, carve-as-planned-settlement (a different social register from the accumulated-work sprawl). Implemented: `carveRanks` — lane + 2-4 units (3-4 wide × 4-5 deep rings, cores stay wall = wrap-family slots for phase 2; 40% C-variant open on the far side; door punched per unit through the gap row), whole footprint validated fresh-wall + unprotected, attach = backward probe from lane start. Camp roll 30% (lane starts beyond the gallery span, offsets 10/12/15 — first anchor sat inside the gallery's own open span, always failed) + standalone **Ranks** button. Verified: 3/24 camps place ranks, standalone works, 0 sealed. Unit cores join wrap centers as phase-2 feature slots.
    - **CALIBRATION ROUND 6, 2026-08-08 (user's seventh drawing): SPARSENESS — the comb vocabulary DISAGGREGATED.** The drawing is scattered fragments at hermit scale: a lone ring, a small knot, spurs, loosely linked with real distance between. Implemented as the sprawl table's rare jackpot (the Red Rock Weight-1-shrine idiom): **lone cell** — a single ring unit alone in the wall (carveRanks forced to 1 unit), 8% of sprawl rolls, front-adjacency connects it. Sprawl table rebalanced: nibble 32 / slot 14 / notch 27 / stepped 11 / island 8 / cell 8. Verified 30 zones: 5 lone cells (~1 in 6 zones), 0 sealed. The register spectrum is now complete: sprawl bites (accumulated work) → lone cells (hermit) → comb (organic warren) → ranks (planned settlement) — one vocabulary, four social densities.
    - **WEAVE + GUARANTEE + MOTIF MIX, 2026-08-08 (user: "weave these into each other and secure that something other than the main living core spawns... break up the blueprints into parts to mix and match"):** (a) **guarantee ladder** — secondary rolls (sleep 45 / comb 60 / ranks 30) now fall back to a forced comb→ranks→sleep attempt when all miss: every full camp gets ≥1 structure beyond the living core (22/28; the rest are honest thin-band core-onlys or waystops); at exactly one structure, 50% reaches for a SECOND. (b) **territorial fix** — the placers were starving each other (identical seam offsets; 0 multi-structure camps in 28): comb spots extended deeper both flanks, ranks tries both flanks → **7/28 multi-structure camps** incl. double-combs. (c) **weave pass** — structures link to their neighbors by extra spines (≤26 apart, 75%, max 2), the star becomes a cyclic network (18/28 camps); spines now REFUSE kept walls (posts/islands/rank-cores registered) so a blocked weave dead-ends honestly instead of gutting a room. (d) **motif mix** — carveComb's parts (rooms/wraps/teeth/stubs) are no longer all-rolled-always: each comb draws 2-4 parts from a weighted menu and logs them (`[rooms+wraps]`, `[stubs+teeth]`...) — the user's blueprint-parts framing made structural; districts within one camp differ in character. Verified 30 zones: 0 sealed throughout.
    - **SEALED-DONUT BUGFIX, 2026-08-08 (user reroll verdict #1: "lone disconnected 3x3/3x4 rooms with a pillar in the middle"):** the wrap move never carved a connector back to the walk — rings floated sealed unless the walk happened to brush them. Fix: wraps must find a DOOR CANDIDATE (ring cell orthogonally adjacent to walk-visited lattice, outside the skip arc) BEFORE carving — no candidate, no wrap — and the connector carves after the ring. Wrap rate 11→4 per ~20 combs (strict but every one reachable). **Plus a permanent ORPHAN TRIPWIRE:** the shell pass runs a fresh post-dress flood from the mouth and counts unreachable camp cells per camp (first version reused the pre-dress flood and false-flagged every alcove). Verified 30 zones: 28/28 camps at 0 orphans, 0 sealed. This class of bug can no longer ship silently.
    - **DEEP PASS + BACK-PASSAGES, 2026-08-08 (user reroll verdicts #2-3: "very few quarry sites are interconnected... huge swathes of plain stone where we could be doing a bit more"):** root cause — everything carved clung to the corridor's skin. (a) **Deep pass** (new genZone stage): 2-4 structures planted in genuinely deep mass (no open floor within 3) — pocket 35 / small comb 30 / deep gallery 20 / lone cell 15 — each tunneled to the nearest daylight (spiral search ≤22) and 80% chained to its neighbor: shafts into darkness, the DF-mine read. Deep combs may carve unattached — the shaft is their door. (b) **Back-passages**: sprawl records its placed workings (locality-sorted); consecutive pairs ≤12 apart link behind the face at 40% — quarry sites form districts. Verified 25 zones: 66 deep sites (~2.6/zone), 18 chains, 10 back-passages, 0 orphans, 0 sealed.
    - **THE DIGGER, 2026-08-08 (user: "is this a good case for the drillbot to actually work for construction? some generations that just had one making patterns"):** agent-based construction — one simulated digger whose TRACE is the generation. (Clarification banked: Qud's Drillbot is only a pathfinder movement profile; this is the stronger behavioral-agent idea, and in-game it's just a loop in the builder — no creature, no AI ticks.) Behavior: momentum-4/curl-2/anti-1 walk preferring fresh rock (35% hesitation re-walking own dig), action rolls — 3x3 lump-socket 6% (the quarrier rhyme), island room 3%, rest pocket 2%, branch-and-return 4% (stack ≤4), turn-around on stall (≤6), respects kept walls + protected shells. Budget 90-150 actions. **Connectivity guaranteed BY CONSTRUCTION — the orphan class cannot occur in a trace.** Standalone Digger button + 30% of zones get one. First stall bug (9-step runs: couldn't re-walk own tunnel, died in first corner) fixed same session. Traces run 76-114 steps, 1-4 sockets, 2-6 branches. Dials noted: room/pocket rates are conservative — tune on verdicts.
    - **SMALL-AND-WINDING RULING, 2026-08-08 (user: "deprioritize large rooms and just figure out how to hone our weird winding hallways and smallish rooms"):** (a) gallery fit-ladder flipped smallest-first, **9x7 grand gallery CUT** — 5x5 colonnade is now the default working, 7x5 the rare big one; (b) comb rooms bias 2x2-dominant while camp lattices LENGTHEN (5-7) — more hall per comb, less chamber; (c) **`windingTunnel` primitive** (noise-cost cardinal Dijkstra, kept-walls near-impassable, shells discouraged-but-doorable) replaces straight L-spines in ALL connective tissue — weave links, deep-site shafts, deep chains, sprawl back-passages. **The camp's main spine stays mason-straight deliberately: the two-voice contrast (chalk-line vs wander) is the ruled design.** Verified 20 zones: 0 orphans, 0 sealed, ~83ms/zone.
    - **FRESH-SLATE RESET, 2026-08-08 (user ruling: "the initial language that's baked in is kind of poisoning what we're trying now — generate it on a fresh blank slate, then try generating it with canyons, then generate it painted [= decorated, has items]"):** the legacy camp chain (mouth→home→spine→gallery→face, the session's oldest code) is recognized as an inherited skeleton every zone still wears under the patches. **Three-stage rebuild plan: (1) BLANK SLATE — the honed language composing alone, DELIVERED as `growWarren`/Warren button:** network growth with no chain — ports (frontier cells), winding hall from a port, small part rolled at its end (room 22 / mini-wrap 16 / ring unit 14 / mini comb 14 / pocket 14 / socket pair 10 / honest dead-end 10), 18% loop-backs between ports, 12-18 parts per warren. First rolls: 34-45 halls, 4-9 loops, full part spread — reads as the user's drawings 1-2 directly. **(2) IN CANYON — same generator constrained by real bands/mouths (next sitting, after stage-1 verdicts). (3) PAINTED — dressed with items (the deferred phase-2 tables).** The old Zone pipeline stays intact for comparison; it is now the LEGACY track. Verdict cycle: reroll Warren, judge the pure language, tune, then advance stages.
    - **CALIBRATION ROUNDS 7-8, 2026-08-08 (user's eighth + ninth drawings, "two more examples to refine with") — first rounds tuned against the FRESH warren:** drawing 8 gave the local grammar — **hooks** (G-spiral stubs curling around a wall pocket, 5% part roll) and **footed dead-ends** (stubs that turn once at the tip, replacing flat dead-ends). Drawing 9 gave a second HALL VOICE — **trunks** (30% of halls): long (12-24), mostly straight with sparse 15% single jogs — the staircase-step hand, purposeful travel vs the local wander; trunk convergence produces drawing 9's central-knot structure emergently. Verified: all features fire (5-16 trunks, 1-4 hooks, 0-3 feet, 0-11 loops per warren). Corpus now nine drawings; the warren speaks both the near hand and the far hand.
    - **NO-TUNNEL-WITHOUT-A-REASON, 2026-08-08 (user verdict: "warren still generates a lot of empty, long spaces rather than tunnels-into-rooms — but I might be diluting the thing that makes it feel like a stonecarver's quarry"):** the design answer, banked — **tunnels-into-rooms is dungeon logic; no-tunnel-without-a-reason is quarry logic.** A dungeon's halls deliver destinations; a quarry's halls exist because every foot cost labor — the threat to quarry-feel is purposelessness and REGULARITY of payoff, not rooms per se. Built: (a) **every hall ends in work** — failed part rolls fall back room→socket→prospector's-foot (an abandoned test-dig is also work); (b) **trunks are haulage-ways** — 12%/step side-bites along their length (loading bays), length trimmed 12-24→10-20; (c) local halls trimmed 4-10→4-8. Effect: halls-to-parts ratio collapsed ~3:1 → ~1:1 (12-17 halls now yield 15-17 parts vs 34-45 halls for the same parts before) — the bare-hall bloat is gone while payoff KIND stays irregular (chamber / sockets / feet). The quarry-ness lives in work-evidence + extraction causality + fit-rule irregularity, all preserved.
    - **CALIBRATION ROUND 9, 2026-08-08 (user's tenth drawing, "constrained rooms"): the SUITE** — a bounded footprint (7-10 × 5-7, strict all-wall validation) whose interior is subdivided into 2-4 small rooms by shared 1-cell partitions, doors punched per partition, ONE entrance from the arriving hall. The motif the warren lacked: rooms PACKED against each other (domestic architecture behind a door) vs the isolated dilations everywhere else. 14% part roll (part table rebalanced: room 16 / suite 14 / wrap 14 / unit 12 / comb 10 / pocket 12 / sockets 10 / hook 6 / foot 6); fires in ~1/3 of warrens (footprint is demanding — fits where the network left space). Dial noted: feet counts run high (7-10/warren — the work-guarantee ladder lands on prospector's-feet often in dense networks); tune if the user reads it as stubby. Corpus: ten drawings.
    - **PROTECTED SHELL added same session (user: "central areas spawn with a third of their walls gone"):** root cause — pocket walls validated at carve time but eaten afterward: sprawl bites tolerate 15-30% open footprint (can punch through a 1-cell pocket wall), and the pinch police would happily OPEN a diagonal pinch between pocket interior and corridor. Fix: genQuarry collects every camp-chain cell (home/threshold/spine/gallery/face/alcoves/comb/bore/digs/back-door), marks interiors `protectedFloor` + all adjacent walls `protectedShell` (8-dir, ~70-150 walls/camp); sprawl bites reject any footprint touching either; police never clears shell walls. Verified 30 zones: 29 camps, 0 sealed, pockets stay interior.
    - **WARREN LAB BUILT 2026-08-11 — `design/warren-lab.html`, the fresh-slate track's dedicated rig** (standalone carve-focused fork of tile-sketch; own localStorage keys `tfwarren*`, tile-sketch canvases untouched). What it adds over tile-sketch: **46 knobs in 7 groups** (every formerly-hardcoded generator number; live reroll, "Reset all" = the 2026-08-08 tuned baseline verified byte-identical, "Copy diff" emits only changed knobs; part-table weights are real weights now, not a cumulative R(1,100) if-chain); **roll history in its own list** that brush strokes never truncate (fixes the v1 lost-roll bug), each entry storing seed AND the knob values it rolled under — a landed roll is reproducible, not just a picture; **vocabulary gallery** — 23 cards, one per primitive, each rendered by the generator's OWN carve function on a scratch grid (cards cannot drift from code), role-tag filters, per-card local persistent reference-image slot; **Qud tiles toggle** — walls masked off neighbours exactly as `ZoneManager.PaintWall` does, floors rolling `sw_ground_dots` variants, kept walls (standing cores / phase-2 slots) amber-tinted. Legacy camp chain kept behind a collapsed disclosure. Sprites live in gitignored `design/tiles/walls/` (Freehold's assets — the repo carries `extract-qud-walls.py`, the recipe, not the art; lab degrades gracefully if the folder is empty). Extraction path documented in the script: regenerate Unity type trees from `Assembly-CSharp.dll` via UnityPy's TypeTreeGenerator, deserialize the ~27k `exTextureInfo` index, crop from the `Kobold_DynamicAtlas_Walls*` pages; tint baked in (file:// canvas taint), `--body/--detail/--swap` to reskin. **UNVERIFIED Claude inference: black=body(TileColor) / white=detail(DetailColor)** — chosen because black is ~78% of pixels and shale should read mostly red; not confirmed in the shader; `--swap` if inverted vs the real game.
    - **GROUND TRUTH CORRECTED 2026-08-11 (re-decompiled against the current Steam install — decompiled fact, not proposal). These change generated shape:** (1) **the Drillbot's noise is not a smooth cost field** — `AddNoise` builds `NoiseMap(80,25,10, 3,3, 4, 80,80, 6, 3,-3, 1)` then sets weight = FLAT 98 for every cell whose noise exceeds 1 (~76% of the zone), sub-cutoff cells keeping terrain weight (solid wall = real obstacles); a canyon is a shortest path through ~21% blob obstacles — long clean runs bending hard around blobs, NOT the fine-grained wobble of the 2026-08-07 reconstruction's `1 + white*6`. (2) **Stairs count as connections** — CanyonBuilder paths to every Stairs connection as well as every Canyon one, and those endpoints are interior: real canyons grow spurs to mid-zone staircases; the sim never modelled this. (3) **The 4×4 hub clearing happens only on the Start branch**, and with a Canyon Start connection the hub sits AT that connection (an edge cell), not at a random interior point. (4) **`GetLocalAdjacentCells(r)` is a filled Chebyshev box clamped to 0..W-1/0..H-1** — widening reaches the zone edge, it does not stop at the border ring. (5) `Stat.Random(1, high)` rolled once per path cell: confirmed unchanged. (6) **`PaintWall` mask order is N NE E SE S SW W NW**, off-map neighbours read 0 via `bEdgeFlag`; a neighbour connects on same-blueprint, same `PaintWith`, `PaintWith == "*"`, or having the `PaintPart` part (same convention as `Fx_StoneRise.ComposeTile`/`ISOLATED_MASK`). (7) **All 256 `wall_rock` masks are stocked** (counted in the tile index) — no carve shape can hit the blank-`InvalidInfo` path. (8) **Village hut floors are `DirtPath` on every interior cell**, hardcoded in `VillageMaker` AND `VillageBase` for every faction — goatfolk identity is `ThatchedWall` + `RoundBuildings` + population, not floors; `DirtPath` inherits `DirtFloor` with `RandomTileOnMove` over four `sw_ground_dots` variants. **Qud's own idiom: walls autotile off neighbours, floors roll one of N** — governs any floor work we do.
    - **THREE BUGS FOUND + FIXED 2026-08-11 — two were corrupting the design record:** (1) **the mini comb had NEVER fired in a warren** — `carveComb` demands an all-wall footprint but the call site centred it on the cell the hall had just carved, so the check failed on the hall itself every time (20 warrens with comb as the ONLY table part: zero combs). Now placed ahead of the hall end like the suite; fires ~1 warren in 3. ~~The same bug is still LIVE in `tile-sketch.html` (identical call, line ~1208) — port-pending.~~ **PORTED TO tile-sketch 2026-08-11 (vine sitting): both the comb call-site fix AND the ranks attach-flag revert (the second was also live there — growWarren originated in tile-sketch, so it carried both). Verified in-browser: 21 rolls, combs land in 6 (~1 in 3.5, was zero-possible), unattached combs/units carve-then-revert in the log.** (2) **66% of rank units were sealed** — `carveRanks` reports whether its lane reached open floor and `growWarren` ignored the flag (15 unit-only warrens: 57 of 86 accepted units were ones carveRanks itself had logged UNATTACHED). Now reverts like the comb; units drop ~6/warren → ~1. **Any ranks judgement made before 2026-08-11 was made on two-thirds-broken output.** (3) gallery ring-unit demo card drew its lane and depth axes parallel (a bar, not a unit) — gallery-only, no generator impact.
    - **DIAGONAL SEAL — USER RULING 2026-08-11 (vine sitting, judging a positive-example warren roll; the flag: a room enterable through a corner-kiss with the hall — "there's a diagonal section where you can walk into the room - but if we just fill that from inside the room, then you can still walk down the hallway without even noticing it was there in the first place"). BUILT into warren-lab same sitting:** Qud walks diagonals, so a chamber corner diagonally touching outside floor IS a door nobody dug; the rule seals it from the CHAMBER side — the hall keeps its line, the room goes quiet behind its real entrance (discovery, not announcement — rooms you walk past without knowing). Implementation: `sealChamberDiagonals` — chamber-family parts (room / suite / wrap / ring unit / comb / pocket + fallback rooms) seal at birth against pre-existing floor, then one final union sweep catches halls carved past older rooms; doorway guard (a cell orthogonally open to outside floor is never sealed), and every fill runs an 8-dir connectivity flood (Qud movement rules) reverting any fill that would strand a cell — a room whose ONLY entrance was the leak keeps the leak. Work-bites (sockets/hooks/feet) stay open to the hall by design. **Inversion note: the legacy grammar police OPENS diagonal pinches (Ruiner idiom) — that law still governs the legacy track; the seal is warren-only.** New knob `sealDiag` (warren — grammar group, default ON) for A/B against old behavior. Verified 30 rolls: seals fire in ~30% of warrens (8 rolls ×1, 1 roll ×2), 0 stranded cells (independent flood check on every export), 0 errors. Port-pending to tile-sketch + eventual ZonePainter per the sim-first workflow.
    - **HIDDEN GEOLOGY / WET POCKETS — USER CONCEPT 2026-08-11 (same camp jam, answering Claude's "no geology events" critique of a warren roll): "3x3 squares (or really any shape) full of pools of salty water, but all the walls around it secure - so a player wouldn't see the pools directly (cave walls are high), but they could see what surrounds it - cave moss, small flora, muddy soil, leaks from the wall, puddles." Irregular-interior refinement same sitting ("the avoidance pockets, internally, do not need to be a perfect square"). BUILT into warren-lab same sitting, knobs in a new "warren — geology" group:** one sealed brine pocket per warren at `wetChance` (def 0.45 — iteration-friendly, final rarity is a USER DIAL), size 2-3 per axis with 40% corner drops (never gutted below 3 cells), anchored 6-14 cells off the warren start so the network grows toward it; pool + 1-ring shell registered protected across every carve path (comb/blob/wrap gained explicit guards — latent holes anyway), tunnels refuse pool/shell at hard cost, and a soft `wetAvoid` ring (CLAUDE dial, def on at 8) makes halls conspicuously bend around the wet ground — the avoided-mass read. Symptoms are dress-phase (`gendress` only; bare stage-1 geometry shows just the sealed water + the bend): distance-gradient halo — full kit pressed near the shell (moss/seep/mud/≤2 puddles), thinning to stray mud at reach (`wetHalo`), one guaranteed tell if the rolls all miss (a symptomless pocket is indistinguishable from rock). New palette terrain: muddy soil `;` + cave moss `%` (seep reuses the existing trough object). **Verified 40 dressed rolls: 13 pockets, 13/13 sealed (blob-level breach check on every export), 13/13 irregular, 13/13 symptomatic, 0 disconnects.** Watch item: one 1/30 disconnect flag in an early run, unreproduced across 100 subsequent checked rolls — likely checker artifact. **Convergences noted at the sitting (Claude, for design leverage): Geomancy's reveal-through-stone is the natural prospecting verb for hidden pockets · wet moss = the moss-boxes supply chain (morestonecarver.txt float) · brine pools = what wild pig vessels graze back to (the re-dilution behavior the boar ruling wrote) · SALTY is the economy guard (fresh water in walls would be currency) · pickaxe players CAN breach — symptoms as treasure map, mild reward by construction. OPEN, user's to rule: final rarity · a rare pre-breached variant (flooded working, the one time they didn't avoid it — CLAUDE float) · symptom flora specifics at the C# port (base blueprint picks).** Port-pending with the seal.
      - **MOSS = WALL-MOUNTED, RULED same sitting (user: "i think the rock should spawn on the wall? that way we can just cheaply recolor it when it's time to harvest? and then just have an item for the harvested bit" · "can we just recolor the wall tile directly if it has it?").** Floor-moss tile CUT from the palette (existed ~minutes); sim now marks `mossyWalls` on open-faced walls in the halo gradient and recolors the ACTUAL wall sprite — no overlay, mirroring the game shape. **RECOLOR THROUGH THE QUD PALETTE (user direction, same sitting: "just add support for these colors in our tester" + color picks "tile color could be #155352 detail #009403 maybe? so it's greyish with a dark green highlight" — those hexes ARE the engine's `K` and `g`, so the blueprint says `TileColor="&K" DetailColor="g"`):** warren-lab gained the 16-code `QUDCOLORS` table (K/g corroborated by the user's hexes; rest = the standard modding-wiki palette, spot-check any code before shipping art on it) + `ensureQudFilter` (SVG luminance-threshold discrete-transfer filter — maps the baked two-tone sprite to EXACT target hexes per (tile, detail) pair, no canvas taint) + `mossTile`/`mossDetail` knob dropdowns (geology group, string-valued sel via a new `str` knob flag) so the moss look iterates live in palette-legal codes only. User's K/g marked "maybe" — provisional pick, the knobs exist to settle it. **XML-ONLY VERDICT (base-XML recon same sitting): the whole game-side behavior is attribute-driven on the stock `Harvestable` part** — `OnSuccess`/`OnSuccessAmount` (the moss item), `DestroyOnHarvest="false"` (wall stays; Lagroot precedent, Creatures.xml:6175), `Ripe*/Unripe*` color triplets (**the cheap recolor is native — one blueprint, two color states**), custom `HarvestVerb`. Not plant-bound: banana trees (ZoneTerrain.xml:91) + furniture (Furniture.xml:2242) carry it, and etching already proved wall twiddle menus. Build = mossy-wall blueprint + moss item blueprint, ZERO new C#; placement rides the wet-pocket painter port. Caveats on file: harvest is gated by the Harvestry skill (design consequence to accept or route around) · re-ripen-after-harvest timer = one decomp check at build. Moss item doubles as the moss-boxes supply (edible, slight taste effect — the morestonecarver float).
    - **STAGE 2 (WARREN IN CANYON) — ATTACHMENT RULES, proposal sitting 2026-08-11. Design only, nothing built yet.** Core frame (CLAUDE PROPOSAL, accepted by the rulings below): `growWarren` learns nothing about canyons per se — canyon floor becomes its own cell class, and the one new law is halls REFUSE canyon floor except at deliberate mouths (the wet-pocket/protected-shell guard shape, third use); the thin-band fold is expected to emerge from refusal alone (corridor forbidden + zone edge = only the seam has mass), so NO band-steering gets pre-engineered — lay a faithful canyon, mask it, one mouth, roll twenty, judge first. Forks ruled at the sitting: **(1) RULED (user: "agree with this lean") — one mouth per warren + ~30% back door**, carrying over the articulation batch's lived-in-burrows-have-two-exits; multiple free ports rejected for trading away the discovery quality the diagonal seal protects. **(2) RULED (user: "this sounds fine") — a warren never crosses the corridor**; a band is one warren's territory, both-bands-inhabited = two warrens. **(3) RULED (user: "agreed") — wet pockets and deep sites FAIL HONESTLY in bands too thin for their depth requirements** (pocket anchor 6-14, deep-pass isolation 3), spawn-nothing-fallback spirit; no shrink-to-fit (a pocket without room for its halo is a puddle). **(4) RULED same sitting (user counter-direction "we can integrate these spurs maybe?" → Claude integration proposal → user: "i like the shape of that ruling") — SPURS AS ATTACHMENT SURFACE:** mouth candidates = wall skin of the whole canyon system (corridor + spurs); spur floor stays refused to non-mouth halls (integration = attachment, not exception — the breach law survives as one law over a bigger surface); mild spur-mouth preference (~2:1 per cell, CLAUDE DIAL — enough to read as a habit, corridor mouths stay unsurprising) for the pilgrim-road fiction (lore ruling: drawn to deep caves — the staircase is the draw). Accepted consequences, riding unruled: stairs unblockable BY CONSTRUCTION (refusal never removes connectivity, mouths only add) · spur-mouthed warren + ~30% corridor back door = emergent through-warren shortcut between corridor and staircase (user saw it flagged, shape accepted — revisit rarity only if play says so) · staircase wraps emerge from seam-folding, no special code. Standing note: `CanyonMarker` survival stays unverified but gates only the eventual ZonePainter port — the lab generates its own canyon. **BUILT + VERIFIED same day (warren-lab lab pass; user: "let's build our lab pass"):** new **Canyon Warren** button (`genCanyonWarren`) composing the faithful canyon gen + `growWarren` untouched — canyon floor becomes a masked cell class, every wall 8-touching it (Qud walks diagonals) is SKIN registered in `keepWalls`+`protectedShell` (the wet-shell idiom's third use — every carver refuses it for free); mouth picker = skin cells with walkable non-water canyon face + `mouthDepth` (def 3) solid rock behind, spur-facing candidates weighted `spurPull` (def 2); `windingTunnel` gains hard canyon refusal PLUS a pre-carve crossing belt (a Dijkstra path forced across the canyon carves NOTHING instead of stranding a fragment on the far side); back door per the ruling (`backDoor` def 0.30, `backDoorMin` def 10, sites = skin between warren floor and non-water canyon floor); wet pockets keep stage-1 anchoring with a new same-band guard (cross-corridor anchors rejected — a pocket the warren can't approach is god-view-only); knobs in a new "warren — canyon (stage 2)" group; both openings render as `>`. Two permanent TRIPWIRES per roll: breach count over remaining skin (expect 0) and warren-side reach (flood from mouth refusing canyon floor must hit every warren cell). **Verified 30 rolls: 30/30 clean — 0 breaches, 0 disconnects, 0 JS errors; spur mouths 11/30 at pull 2; parts avg 14 (med 15, min 5 — thin-band squeezes are honest, not failures); wet 8 placed / 2 honest no-fit fails. Showcase read (seed 200, ascii export): spur mouth → halls fold down the band around the sealed pool → back door beside the staircase slot — the pilgrim through-route EMERGED from the two ruled laws touching, zero steering code.** Two stage-1 fixes found en route: **(1) `carveWrap` carved its DOOR cell unguarded** — a ring that validated clean could still punch its door through a protected shell (wet-pocket or canyon skin); door cell now validated. **Same hole is presumably live in `tile-sketch.html` (growWarren's origin) — port-pending with the seal.** (2) **`sealChamberDiagonals`'s strand check rewritten global→relative** (`allOpenReachable` → before/after `reachCount` comparison): the old law compared against ALL open cells, so a canyon marsh's legitimately-isolated CA ponds would have silently reverted every seal in the zone; the honest question is whether THIS fill stranded anything. Dials flagged for user judgment, all CLAUDE numbers: back door lands ~3/11 rolled (legal sites ≥10 from the mouth are scarce in thin bands — `backDoorMin` is the dial if more through-routes wanted) · feet still run high (avg 8.1, the stage-1 flag unchanged) · sub-10-part rolls in skinny bands (spawn-nothing-adjacent; judge whether a 5-part warren reads as a hermit hole or noise). **VERDICT ROUND 1 same day (user, three screenshot reads: ex1 "warren is too small" · ex2 "warren has too much empty space" · ex3 "decent size and shape warren, but a bit too early to tell") — diagnosis: the ATTEMPT + PAYOFF ECONOMY still assumed a blank slate. Fixes, all gated on canyon presence so stage-1 keeps its exact rng sequence (drawing calibration NOT re-rolled; sanity batch confirms stage-1 numbers byte-identical):** (a) attempt economy — blocked trunks fall through to a local hall in the same attempt, hall targeting re-rolls direction/length ≤4x (doomed targets no longer burn whole attempts); (b) payoff economy — the ROLLED part tries all four rotations before the fallback ladder, and the fallback chamber tries rotations before degrading to a foot (chamber carvers only ever faced the hall's own direction — facing skin, they failed into feet). Measured 101-130 before→after: parts min 5→12 (avg 14→15.1, always in the wanted 12-18 band now — ex1's mechanism dead), chambers min 0→~1 (avg 4→4.5 vs stage-1's 5.4), feet avg 9.5→8.5 (vs stage-1 6.2 — remaining gap is honest space reality, dial if it still reads stubby). Phantom loop count fixed in passing (crossing-belt empty paths no longer logged as loops). **PLUS A TRIPWIRE CATCH, the guard system paying for itself: seed 117 rolled 2 BREACHES — the comb and ranks ATTACH PROBES carve their connector UNGUARDED (no keepWalls/protectedShell check on intermediates, any non-wall accepted as daylight): through canyon skin to canyon floor, and — LATENT IN STAGE 1 — through the wet shell to pool WATER (a probe hitting brine reads it as open floor and unseals the pocket), and through kept posts/rank cores the shard police protects. Both probes fixed (guarded wall = not this way; canyon/pool floor = not daylight); 60-roll re-verify 60/60 clean incl. seed 117. The probe holes are presumably LIVE IN tile-sketch.html (both carvers exist there) — port-pending with the seal + the carveWrap door guard.** Stage-2 re-judge queued: user re-rolls against the fixed economy (ex3's "too early to tell" stands as the open verdict).
    - **MOUTH MASS WEIGHTING + TRIPWIRE CATCH #2, 2026-08-12 (user anti-example verdict while judging a stage-2 batch: "the cave is going to the sparsest section instead of the full areas below" — seed 319, a thin-band warren over a huge untouched mass).** Diagnosis: the mouth picker's only mass check was `mouthDepth` (3 solid cells straight behind, pass/fail); past that every skin cell weighed 1 (×spurPull), and a long thin band offers MORE skin cells than a compact deep mass — the picker was mildly biased toward exactly the wrong rock, and thin-band mouths are foot factories (319 rolled 10 feet of 14 parts). **RULED (user: "let's try that first fix"): mouth candidates are now weighted by the carvable mass behind them** — BFS through wall only (skin refused, same as every carver) within Chebyshev `mouthMassR` (new stage-2 knob, def 7; 0 = flat pre-2026-08-12 weights), weight = mass × spurPull. Verified 30 rolls: chosen mouths average 72% of the zone's best candidate mass, 28/30 in the top half; all-thin zones still produce honest thin-band warrens (weight, not filter — the hermit question stays open). **TRIPWIRE CATCH #2 en route (re-aimed mouths made seeds 304/311 trip the reach wire — bug EXPOSED by the change, latent since the wet-pocket build):** `windingTunnel`'s crossing belt only checked canyon cells, but Dijkstra pays ANY 4000 toll when that's the only route — a path could THREAD THE WET POCKET'S KEPT SHELL (or pool water), the carve step silently skips kept walls, and the tunnel opens on both sides of an intact wall: a sealed fragment with ZERO breaches (seed 304: the mouth pocket was hemmed by the pool, so all 12 halls paid the shell toll and the entire warren grew as an unreachable fragment north of the pool, reach 6/24, pool still sealed). Fixes, both the belt idiom: (a) the belt now rejects paths threading kept walls / pool water / wet shell — such a path carves NOTHING and the attempt economy retries elsewhere; (b) sibling hole in `carveTrunk`: its walk broke only on protected WALLS, so open canyon floor (reachable via the mouth cell, which is unregistered) and pool water never stopped it — a trunk could stroll across the corridor and resume carving on the far side; the walk now breaks at any cell it may neither carve nor claim. Re-verified: stage-2 30/30 clean incl. 304/311 (304 now collapses honestly to a 4-part squeeze — its pool-hemmed mouth pocket really is that small); stage-1 20 rolls, 0 errors, feet avg 6.2→5.8 (the drift = ex-buggy pool-wading trunks now break early; those rolls were broken output, not calibration). **Port-pending to tile-sketch with the rest of the seal batch: the threading hole is presumably LIVE there** (windingTunnel + trunks exist; kept posts/rank cores are threadable the same way; no wet pockets to worry about). Dials flagged (CLAUDE observations): 304-class rolls (a pool eating a cramped mouth's growing room) are the hermit-vs-noise question wearing a new hat · spurPull ×2 can still outvote mass toward a poor spur mouth (seed 304 chose 45-of-127 mass) — dial if it recurs · the foot-heavy fallback ladder remains the OTHER half of the feet problem, unruled (stage-2 feet avg 8.5 after this fix; the mass weighting attacked placement, not fallback).
      - **CLASS-AWARE MOUTH FITTING — the predicted recurrence arrived and got its fix, RULED 2026-08-12 (user verdict on seed 16: "this current seed has a *lot* of negative space" — diagnosis: the weighted mouth lottery drew a mass-16 pocket with 169 on offer, starving a medium warren into a 90-cell scribble with 8-of-15 feet, wet pocket AND gallery both honest-failing in the sliver; Claude offered relative-mass-floor vs class-aware placement, user: "i'll go with your lean").** Build: the size class rolls BEFORE the mouth (extracted `rollWarrenClass`/`classFromBand`; growWarren takes an optional pre-rolled class, stage-1 unchanged), a mouth is eligible for the class only if `mass >= partsWanted x mouthMassPerPart` (new knob, def 7 — CLAUDE dial, the observed rock-consumed-per-part footprint), and when no candidate feeds the class it DEGRADES large → medium → small before anything carves — the rock decides what fits; a small warren in a sliver is a hermit hole, a medium one is a bug. Last resort (all candidates below even small's need): the best pocket digs anyway as a cramped hermit dig — spawn-nothing stays reserved for zero candidates (CLAUDE call). `mouthMassR = 0` disables fitting with the weighting, preserving the legacy A/B. **Verified 30 rolls: 30/30 tripwires clean, degradation fired 10/30 (thin bands stepping down honestly), 0 cramped-dig fallbacks, avg chosen mouth 84% of zone-best mass (was 72% under pure weighting; the seed-16 failure mode was 9%). Seed 16 itself reborn: mass-164-of-169 mouth, large class, 20/20 parts, gallery + wet pocket both fitting, 193 padded cells vs the 90-cell scribble.** Consequence noted: stage-2 rng order changed (class draws before mouth), stage-2 seeds re-roll — deliberate, the fix IS a placement change.
    - **RIVER/MARSH DECOMP-VERIFIED + TRIPWIRE CATCH #3, 2026-08-12 (user, judging a marsh roll: "is water generation broken or is this normal?" → ruling "let's make sure river is accurate to base game decomp").** The river layer was the last piece of the canyon still running on the 2026-08-07 reconstruction (its own code comment said so). Re-decompiled `RiverBuilder` + `Genkit.CellularGrid` via `ilspycmd` against the Steam install (recipe: `ilspycmd -t <TypeName> ".../Caves of Qud/CoQ_Data/Managed/Assembly-CSharp.dll"` — same dll the sprite extractor uses). **Verdict on the user's screenshot: the zone-wide pond splat is FAITHFUL** — marsh CA really does cover the whole zone, enclosed-in-rock ponds included (in game they'd be invisible behind high walls; the lab's god-view exaggerates). **Five reconstruction drifts found and fixed in the sim:** (1) CA rule was born≥5/survive≥4, real is **born {6,7,8} / survive {5,6,7,8}** — the game's marsh is sparser and blobbier than the sim's was; (2) real CA forces the border ring DEAD every pass (SeedBorders=false), sim let seeded edges live; (3) real grid is **80x30 with only rows 1..23 stamped** (bottom rows are live neighbors, not a border); (4) **the lone-mouth marsh zone still gets its river channel** — pathed from a random interior point (5-75, 5-20) out to the lone mouth, the river rising out of the marsh; the sim had marsh replace the river entirely; (5) channel cost is `|simplex(x/3,y)|*190 + 20/wall` — the abs-fold makes rivers follow noise valleys, x stretched 3:1; sim now folds its value noise to match (value noise itself stays a stand-in for FastNoise simplex). Marsh mechanism confirmed: fires iff the zone has EXACTLY ONE river connection (river dead-ends here — world-map topology); `riverChance`/`marshChance` knobs stay as stand-ins for that topology, help text updated. Also confirmed: marsh = `ClearWalls` + `SaltyWaterDeepPool` per cell (salty ✓ the economy guard), `Cell.ClearTerrain` DOES remove walls (channel cuts rock), and RiverBuilder contains a delightful dead line spawning a Drillbot creature into the void when BuildTries>5. Verified 100 stage-2 rolls: marsh 8/100, ponds now 1-3 cohesive components (70-150 water cells) channel-linked to a mouth instead of confetti. **TRIPWIRE CATCH #3 en route (seed 464, exposed by the river resequencing rng): a hall pathed ALONG THE ZONE BORDER ROW** — `noisyPath` must allow the border (canyon mouths live there), but every carver's `inB` and the reach tripwire exclude it, so a hall routed via y=H-1 carved legally yet sat under the flood's floor: 8 "stranded" cells that were really connected through the ring. Fix, belt idiom again: border ring priced 4000 in `windingTunnel`'s cost AND belt-rejected — a hall that can only reach its target along the zone seam carves nothing. The reach tripwire also gained a stranded-cell coordinate dump on failure (hunting this took three tracers; the next hunt starts with coordinates for free). Re-verified: **stage-2 100/100 clean, feet avg 8.5→7.8** (border-crawler halls were junk miles), stage-1 20 rolls 0 errors. Port-pending: the border-ring hole exists wherever tile-sketch's windingTunnel runs.
    - **STAGE 3 FIRST BITE — CANYON FLORA (grass + trees), USER REQUEST 2026-08-12 ("warren v3 having grass and trees painted like the canyon has in game"). GROUND TRUTH PULLED + BUILT INTO warren-lab SAME DAY.** How the game actually does it, all decomp/XML-read today (ilspycmd + StreamingAssets): **(1) the grass is floor PAINT, not objects** — every surface zone's population drops a `Dirty` FloorPainter widget (Widgets.xml:142; `DesertCanyonZoneGlobals` carries its own `<object Blueprint="Dirty"/>` row at PopulationTables.xml:6418) whose `Dirty.PaintCell` runs over EVERY cell in the zone, walls included: d8 → 1-5 brown dirt (`&w`, `Tiles/tile-dirt1.png`, detail `k`) / 6 dark grass `&g` / 7 bright grass `&G` / 8 pale dirt `&y`; grass rolls one of `DirtPicker`'s four tiles (`tile-grass1` + `Terrain/sw_grass1-3`); text mode rolls d5 over `. , ` '` / nothing. Grass never sets a detail color — it lands on `Cell.Render`'s explored-cell default `"k"`, so detail is k across the board (decomp-confirmed, not a guess). **Consequence worth eyes: rock carved open later shows the paint that was always underneath — in game the WARREN floor will grow the same dirt-and-grass, buried greenery included.** The sim shows this faithfully (paint covers all floor); if buried grass reads wrong in play, the C# painter can repaint warren interiors — USER CALL at port time, nothing pre-engineered. **(2) the trees are population objects** — `ZoneTemplate:DesertCanyon` global table `DesertCanyonZoneGlobals` (PopulationTables.xml:6416): Dogthorn Chance 95 Number 30-80, Witchwood 25/5-20, Starapple 5/5-20, scattered on any empty cell; the template is a POSTBUILDER running after carve + river and before any mod post-builder, so trees stand on canyon floor only and the warren (carved after) never gets any — the sim scatters at the canyon generator's tail, exactly that slot. `[verify]` at C# port time: our painter's actual position in the postbuilder order. **Built:** new "canyon — flora (stage 3)" knob group (16 knobs, defaults ARE the game numbers: floor-look select with the old sw_ground_dots stand-in kept as legacy option · the d8 as four weights · glyph chance · chance/min/max per species); floor paint hashes off the ROLL's seed (`paintSeed`, persisted in autosave + history + kept canvases — no shimmer within a roll, fresh ground every reroll; the game rerolls per zone build); grass/dirt sprites extracted as black/white masks and recolored live through `ensureQudFilter` to exact palette codes (the moss path's second customer — zero baked tints); tree palette entries recolored to blueprint glyph truth (dogthorn `&G`, witchwood `&W`, starapple `&R`, ZoneTerrain.xml). `extract-qud-walls.py` gained manifest MERGE with per-tile tint records (a floor run no longer clobbers the walls manifest). **Verified in-browser (glyph mode — the pane's data: snapshot can't load sprite files; tile-mode eyes-on = next time the user opens the lab from disk, the filter path itself is the proven moss machinery):** canyon roll logs `flora: dogthorn 37, witchwood 0, starapple 0` (gates behaving), Canyon Warren end-to-end clean — 64 dogthorn placed BEFORE the skin scan, then mouth pick 22 candidates / 13 parts / 0 tripwire breaches / reach 142/142; 0 JS errors. CLAUDE dials on record: the d5 glyph roll compressed to one chance knob (0.8 = the real 4-in-5) · trees at the canyon tail also ride the legacy Zone chain (faithful — the template dresses those zones too). Sprites remain gitignored per the standing rule; the repo carries the recipe. **FIX ROUND same day (user first-look: "it's not loading tiles?" — tiles WERE loading; the recolor was eating them): CSS `filter` recolors the element's ENTIRE painted output, and the floor cells' near-black background + dark outline both sat below the luminance threshold → mapped to the body color → solid slabs reading as broken tiles. Moss never hit this because wall sprites are full-bleed opaque. Fix: Dirty-floor cells go transparent-background + transparent-outline so the filter only ever sees the mask (lesson comment left at the call site). Two more in the same pass: (1) atlas m_Names carry mixed case (`Tile-Dirt1`) — files renamed lowercase + the extractor now normalizes keys on save (Windows forgives the mismatch, nothing else would); (2) TREES UPGRADED to the game's own masks — the old bg_ pngs are baked in two LIGHT tones (y + detail), which the luminance threshold would flatten to one color, so the three species now extract their real sprites (`sw_fattree1/2`, `sw_talltree1/2`, `sw_starappletree`) and recolor live to blueprint TileColor/DetailColor (dogthorn G/w, witchwood W/g, starapple g/R), variant rolled by coord hash = the blueprints' own RandomTile pair; bg_ pngs stay as the no-tiles fallback. Verified over http this time (new `.claude/launch.json` "warren-lab" config serves design/ on :8123 — the pane's data: snapshot can't load relative sprites, http can): canyon roll renders dark ground + dirt speckle + two-green tufts + green dogthorns with brown trunks against red shale, 0 console errors.**
    - **POCKET CUT FROM THE PART TABLE — USER RULING 2026-08-12 ("can we cut the pocket from the warren sim?"; scope confirmed at the sitting: the part-table pocket primitive, NOT the wet-pocket geology).** `wPocket` baseline 12 → 0; the knob survives so the cut is A/B-able, `carvePocket` survives (the legacy digger track calls it, and the wet-pocket sizing knobs are unrelated machinery), the gallery card stays as vocabulary documentation. One-time flag-guarded localStorage migration (`tfwarren_mig_pocketcut`) rewrites a stored pre-cut baseline 12 to 0 so the ruling lands in live labs without stomping a future deliberate re-raise. NOTE: the "Reset all = 2026-08-08 tuned baseline" claim now carries this one ruled delta (pocket 0). Roll-history caveat, standing rig semantics: flipping back to a pre-cut roll restores that roll's knob snapshot, pocket 12 included — reproduce-the-roll beats the ruling inside history, fresh rolls take the new baseline. Verified 20 fresh warrens over http: 299 parts, 0 pockets, avg 15 parts/warren (the 12-18 band holds — the table renormalized over the remaining eight parts), 0 console errors.
    - **SHARD SWEEP — USER FLAG + RULING 2026-08-12 (screenshot verdict: "larger rooms have a lot of unstructured blocks laying about - how do you feel about that?" → Claude case: accidental shards spend the kept-core vocabulary's credibility (wrap core / island notch / comb posts only read as deliberate when random lumps are rare; the game has no amber tint) and quarry logic pays for every open cell — worked rooms don't keep random lumps; floor interruption in big chambers belongs to quarried-stack dressing + deliberate cores → user: "let's try that").** Probe before: isolated walls (≥6 of 8 neighbours open) per warren ran [22,1,2,0,0,35,11,14,6,3,0,9] — tails far beyond what deliberate cores account for. BUILT into `growWarren`: the legacy police's LONE-shard half only (all 8 neighbours open → cleared; the pinch-OPENING half stays legacy-only, inverted by the diagonal-seal ruling), keepWalls/protectedShell exempt — which covers canyon skin for free, so the drillbot's own corridor islets are untouchable; 2+ cell remnants deliberately stay (they read as stubs of something). Runs BEFORE the seal union so a seal fill is never sweep bait (clearing an 8-open cell cannot create a new corner kiss). Knob `shardSweep` (grammar group, def on) for A/B; count rides the warren log line. Verified: stage-1 isolated tails collapse to [3,0,3,0,7,8,0,0,0,1,0,6] (remainder = exempt cores + stubs), canyon-warren 10/10 clean tripwires / 0 breaches, 0 console errors. Port-pending to tile-sketch + eventual ZonePainter with the rest of the seal batch.
    - **PILLARED GALLERY + WARREN SIZE CLASSES — DRAWING 11 + THREE RULINGS 2026-08-12 (language-sharpening sitting: user "mixed on the current language", Claude gap-list, user answered the no-deliberate-large-space item with a drawing: "something like this?"). BUILT + VERIFIED same day.** The drawing's corrections to Claude's pitch, all encoded: pillars are sparse LONE kept cells loosely shadowing the perimeter (not a grid — gridding is the comb's job; these read as what they didn't dare take), the perimeter does the work (bays + jogged corners — bigness earned bay by bay), and it's a HUB, not a terminating part — multiple ways in, sub-rooms hanging off. Rulings at the sitting: **(1) the three parallel top stubs = BLIND SLOTS** (storage/divot read, not entries); **(2) medium rarity, medium+ warrens only — and "start classifying warrens by small, medium, and large"**; **(3) side boxes = small chambers budding on, existing vocabulary fills the slot.** Build: **size classes** — growWarren rolls small/medium/large (weights 25/50/25, CLAUDE dials) with part bands small 7-11 / medium 12-18 (the calibrated band, untouched) / large 20-28 (small/large bands CLAUDE dials); both callers stopped passing part counts, so old seeds re-roll (deliberate — this is a language extension, not a fix; the ten-drawing calibration lives in the knob values, which are unchanged). **Gallery** (`placeGallery` + resolve in growWarren, `galleryChance` def 0.4 on medium+): interior 10-14 x 5-7 rolled, placed PRE-GROWTH 5-12 cells off the start (wet-pocket idiom inverted — tunnels prefer open space, so the network gets drawn through it with zero steering code), footprint+ring validated against every guarded map; 2-4 jogged corners, 3-5 outward bays (shell-guarded), 4-7 lone pillars in `keepWalls` (the shard sweep's exemption makes every lone block in this room a guaranteed-deliberate read — the sweep and the gallery were built the same day and cash the same credibility), 3 blind slots 1-wide x 2-3 deep with rock between (blind-validated: the cell beyond stays wall, the mouth must open onto gallery floor). **PUBLIC by design: interior never registered in chamberCells — dens go quiet behind one sealed door, the gallery is a crossroads; accepted consequence: diagonal kisses into the gallery stay walkable (it has no privacy to protect).** Resolve after growth: flood-check for natural attachment → up to 2 guarded probe doors from different sides (canyon floor is NOT legal daylight — a gallery door onto the corridor would be a second canyon mouth; pool water refused per the probe lesson) → zero doors = honest revert to rock (cells re-walled, pillar keeps unregistered). Buds after attach: 1-2 rooms via `carveNotch` doored through the perimeter, sealed as private dens. **Verified 130 rolls (stage-1 + 20 canyon-warren):** classes 25/49/26%-ish; gallery attempt rate 40/90 medium+ = 0.44 ≈ the 0.4 knob; **every placed gallery attached by pass-through — 0 probes needed, 0 reverts** (the attraction really is emergent); 20/20 canyon tripwires clean; 0 console errors. Feel-round dials flagged (all CLAUDE): slot count often lands 1-2 of 3 (bays eat the wall band first — dial bay count or slot placement if the triple-stub read matters); bud chance 75% for 1-2; gallery geometry numbers throughout; class weights/bands. Showcase seed 131 (stage-1): 11x7 gallery, 6 pillars, 3 bays, 1 slot, pass-through attach + 1 bud.
    - **STAGE 3 DRESS PASS v1 — WORK EVIDENCE, LIVED-IN TENSE (USER RULINGS 2026-08-12: "we could start with lived in and progress from there" + "work evidence first. i think gallery/communal comes second?"). BUILT + VERIFIED same day.** Architecture decision that shapes the whole phase: **the fresh warren needs NO space-classification machinery — carvers REGISTER each space's identity at birth** (`dressSpaces`: kind + cells, pushed by the socket/foot/trunk-bay call sites as they carve) and `dressWarren` walks the registry rolling one small table per kind. The legacy plan's influence-map inference is dead weight for the fresh track; the eventual ZonePainter port inherits the registry idea. Table laws applied from the redrock recon: heavy Nothing weights, one rare jackpot beside mundane rows, ONE item max per space, placed on a random floor cell of that space. Tables v1 (ALL WEIGHTS CLAUDE DIALS): socket = Nothing 60 / stack-low 20 / torch 8 / chest 2 (tool stash jackpot) · foot = Nothing 70 / stack 12 / torch 10 / figurine 3 (**the maquette at the dead end — the calling's own Random Copper Figurine echo placed by the world**) · bay = Nothing 40 / stacks 18-15-12 by grade (bays stage product) / chalkline 5 / chest 3. Stack rolls pick a variant (base/a/b) per grade. Rides the existing "dress" checkbox (wetHaloDress idiom) — bare geometry stays judgeable. `carveFoot` now returns its cells (truthy array, boolean call sites unaffected). **Verified 20 dressed rolls: 241 work spaces, 59% rolled Nothing (the density-variance law holding), items = 68 stacks (grade-1 heavy) / 16 torches / 6 figurines / 4 chests / 4 chalklines; 0 console errors; undressed rolls untouched (pass no-ops without the checkbox).** **FEEDBACK ROUND same day (USER: "i would add pickaxes, and stray torches, not torchposts, that also generate in the work areas (maybe with adjacency?)") — BUILT:** torchpost OUT of the work tables (palette entry survives for later communal dress), new `straytorch` + `pickaxe` items with real renders (Torch `*` `&w`/R flame detail, Pickaxe `♦` Tool-base y/w — Items.xml read; `sw_torch`/`sw_pickaxe` masks extracted, riding the qtiles recolor path), and the adjacency float landed as the **FACE HINT**: table rows may carry `hint:"face"` placing the item at the space's DEEPEST floor cell — carvers push cells depth-major, so the end of the list IS the working face; light and tools rest where work stopped, the redrock relational idiom at bite scale. Tables now: socket 55N/18stack/8torch/5pick/2chest · foot 66N/10stack/10torch/6pick/3figurine · bay gains pickaxe 4 (stored tool).   - [ ] `[design]` **Compensation armor — USER LEAN, RECORDED NOT SCHEDULED (2026-08-15): "i could see maybe giving them another piece of armor to compensate for the loss (fewer buys at first shop), but i'm not too pressed."** Both conditions are the user's own: it happens **after an audit of what every other calling starts with**, and the item is already chosen — **leather gloves** (base: Commerce 5, AV 1 DV 0, Hands, which is an empty slot in the current kit). Worth seeing the shape of that trade before it happens: +1 AV for +5 trade value restores almost none of the 46 lost, so it would be a genuine armor decision rather than a disguised economy one. Do not pick this up before the audit exists.

 Ground truth that makes the figurine half a one-blueprint item (decomp, `RandomFigurine`): the part resolves rep through a hardcoded material switch (copper 60 / silver 100 / ... / peridot 350) and **any UNKNOWN material takes the default arm = 60 rep, copper's exact value** — so a mod blueprint with `Material="stone"` keeps full carried-rep (`AddsRep` against the depicted creature's whole allegiance list, same as copper) while Commerce is whatever we write; rep is NOT tunable below 60 without C#, which the ruling doesn't ask for. **Wood is mechanically identical (same default arm) — the stone-vs-wood fork is pure fiction and stays OPEN, user's to rule; Claude lean on record: stone (the maquette is the carver's own practice hand; whittling is somebody else's).** Build: foot-table figurine roll is now a material ladder — stone maquette 2 / copper 1 (total weight unchanged at 3; the split is a CLAUDE dial), new `stonefig` palette entry (real figurine mask extracted, y/K stone tint = CLAUDE sketch, mod blueprint TBD at port with the Commerce number owed — copper is 60, "very little" is the user's word). Trash = the base `Garbage` blueprint verbatim (Items.xml:12425 — `%` &K^k, RandomTile over sw_trash_1-4, rummageable via the `Garbage` part, burn-spawns TrashFire): **zero mod cost, base item placed as-is**; rows socket 5 / foot 4 / bay 4 (CLAUDE dials), all four trash masks extracted as coord-rolled variants. DetailColor is unset in the Garbage blueprint — the lab's `k` detail is inference, not decomp. **Verified 40 dressed stage-1 rolls over http: 533 work spaces — 21 trash / 7 stone maquettes / 1 copper (small-sample skew on a weight-3 row; direction correct), Nothing rate 57% holding the density law; 10/10 canyon-warren tripwires clean (dress runs post-carve, geometry untouched by construction); 0 console errors; all five new masks resolving 16x24.** Port checklist addition: `TF Random Stone Figurine` blueprint (Inherits `Random Figurine`, `Material="stone"`, low Commerce) rides the dress-table port; trash needs nothing.

The one-item-per-space law keeps its meaning: one ROLL per space, the pile is that roll's size — and a spent bite filling with junk reads as a midden, extraction causality in the lived-in tense. Coord-hash sprite variants give piles mixed trash for free. Squeeze is honest: tight bites can't fit extras, so measured lone rate runs ~68% against the 55% dial. **Verified 40 dressed rolls: 22 trash placements — 15 lone / 5 pairs / 2 four-piles; 10/10 canyon tripwires clean; 0 errors incl. a 12k-event mouseover sweep across 6 rolls** (run to chase a buffered console error whose line:col only exists in the pre-edit page — stale browser cache, not live code; noted because it burned twenty minutes).
